<sup>31</sup> 「<ruby><rb>所以</rb><rt>Sô-yi</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>人個</rb><rt>Nying-go</rt><rb>百樣</rb><rt>pah-yiang</rt><rb>罪惡</rb><rt>ze-ôh</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>褻瀆個</rb><rt>sih-doh-go</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>赦掉</rb><rt>sô-diao</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>只有</rb><rt>tsih-yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>褻瀆</rb><rt>sih-doh</rt><rb>聖靈</rb><rt>*Sing*-Ling</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>赦弗掉個</rb><rt>sô-feh-diao-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>32</sup> <ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>Væn-pah</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>得罪</rb><rt>teh-ze</rt><rb>人個</rb><rt>Nying-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>Ng-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>赦</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>掉</rb><rt>diao</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>只有</rb><rt>tsih-yiu</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>*shih-wô*</rt><rb>得罪</rb><rt>teh-ze</rt><rb>聖靈</rb><rt>Sing-Ling</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>今世</rb><rt>kying-si</rt><rb>下世</rb><rt>ʽô-si</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>赦弗掉</rb><rt>sô-feh-diao</rt></ruby>。<sup>33</sup> <ruby><rb>一株</rb><rt>Ih-cü</rt><rb>樹</rb><rt>jü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>算</rb><rt>sön</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>好個</rb><rt>hao-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>果子</rb><rt>ko-ts</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>好個</rb><rt>hao-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>一株</rb><rt>ih-cü</rt><rb>樹</rb><rt>jü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>算</rb><rt>sön</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>壞個</rb><rt>w̆a-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>果子</rb><rt>ko-ts</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>壞個</rb><rt>w̆a-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>果子</rb><rt>ko-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>識得</rb><rt>sih-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>樹</rb><rt>jü</rt></ruby>。<sup>34</sup> <ruby><rb>毒蛇個</rb><rt>Doh-dzô-go</rt><rb>種黨</rb><rt>cong-tông</rt></ruby>！<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>既然</rb><rt>kyi-jün</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>惡個</rb><rt>ôh-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>能夠</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>*shih-wô*</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>嘴巴</rb><rt>cü-pô</rt><rb>講個</rb><rt>kông-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>心裏</rb><rt>sing-li</rt><rb>溢出來個</rb><rt>kah-cʽih-læ-go</rt><rb>多頭</rb><rt>to-deo</rt></ruby>。<sup>35</sup> <ruby><rb>好人</rb><rt>Hao-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>裏向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>囥的個</rb><rt>kʽông-tih-go</rt><rb>好處</rb><rt>hao-cʽü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>發出</rb><rt>fah-cʽih</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>壞人</rb><rt>w̆a-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>*sing*</rt><rb>裏向</rb><rt>*li-hyiang*</rt><rb>囥的個</rb><rt>kʽông-tih-go</rt><rb>壞處</rb><rt>w̆a-cʽü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>發出</rb><rt>fah-cʽih</rt><rb>壞</rb><rt>w̆a</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。<sup>36</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>Nying</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>講個</rb><rt>kông-go</rt><rb>虛話</rb><rt>hyü-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>審判</rb><rt>sing-pʽun</rt><rb>日子</rb><rt>nyih-ts</rt><rb>算</rb><rt>sön</rt><rb>賬</rb><rt>tsiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>句打句</rb><rt>kyü-tang-kyü</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>算進</rb><rt>sön-tsing</rt></ruby>。<sup>37</sup> <ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>算</rb><rt>sön</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>正直個</rb><rt>tsing-dzih-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>定</rb><rt>ding</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>罪</rb><rt>ze</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>38</sup> <ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>KEH-TSAO</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>幾個</rb><rt>kyi-go</rt><rb>讀書人</rb><rt>doh-shü-nying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法利賽</rb><rt>Fah-li-sæ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>開</rb><rt>kʽæ</rt><rb>口</rb><rt>kʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>先生</rb><rt>Sin-sang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>顯出</rb><rt>hyin-cʽih</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>兆頭</rb><rt>ziao-deo</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>看看</rb><rt>kʽen-kʽen</rt></ruby>。」<sup>39</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>*Keh-go*</rt><rb>兇惡</rb><rt>hyüong-ôh</rt><rb>姦淫個</rb><rt>kæn-ying-go</rt><rb>世代</rb><rt>shü-dæ</rt><rb>要想</rb><rt>iao-siang</rt><rb>求</rb><rt>gyiu</rt><rb>兆頭</rb><rt>ziao-deo</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>除了</rb><rt>djü-leh</rt><rb>先知</rb><rt>sin-cü</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>約拿</u>個</rb><rt>Iah-nô-go</rt><rb>兆頭</rb><rt>ziao-deo</rt><rb>之外</rb><rt>ts-nga</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>總</rb><rt>tsong</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>*kʽen*</rt><rb>啥個</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>兆頭</rb><rt>ziao-deo</rt></ruby>。<sup>40</sup> <ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>正像</rb><rt>tsing-ziang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>約拿</rb><rt>Iah-nô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>三</rb><rt>sæn</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt><rb>三</rb><rt>sæn</rt><rb>夜</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>鯨魚</rb><rt>gying-ng</rt><rb>肚皮</rb><rt>du-bi</rt><rb>裏向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>人個</rb><rt>Nying-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>Ng-ts</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>三</rb><rt>sæn</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt><rb>三</rb><rt>sæn</rt><rb>夜</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>地垟</rb><rt>di-yiang</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt></ruby>。<sup>41</sup> <u><ruby><rb>尼尼微</rb><rt>Nyi-nyi-vi</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>審判</rb><rt>sing-pʽun</rt><rb>日子</rb><rt>*nyih-ts*</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>現在</rb><rt>yin-dzæ</rt><rb>箇代</rb><rt>keh-dæ</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>*nying*</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>爬起來</rb><rt>bô-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>尼尼微</rb><rt>*Nyi-nyi-vi*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>*nying*</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>定</rb><rt>ding</rt><rb>箇代</rb><rt>keh-dæ</rt><rb>人個</rb><rt>*nying-go*</rt><rb>罪</rb><rt>ze</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>聽</rb><rt>tʽing</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>約拿</u>個</rb><rt>Iah-nô-go</rt><rb>教訓</rb><rt>kao-hyüing</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>回心轉意</rb><rt>we-sing-cün-i</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>況且</rb><rt>hwông-tsʽia</rt><rb>蕩頭</rb><rt>dông-deo</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>比</rb><rt>pi</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>約拿</rb><rt>Iah-nô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>還</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>大個</rb><rt>do-go</rt><rb>來東</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt></ruby>。<sup>42</sup> <ruby><rb>南邊個</rb><rt>Nen-pin-go</rt><rb>女皇</rb><rt>nyü-wông</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>審判</rb><rt>sing-pʽun</rt><rb>日子</rb><rt>nyih-ts</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>現在</rb><rt>yin-dzæ</rt><rb>箇代</rb><rt>keh-dæ</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>*nying*</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>爬起來</rb><rt>bô-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>*keh-go*</rt><rb>女皇</rb><rt>*nyü-wông*</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>定</rb><rt>ding</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>罪</rb><rt>ze</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>地個</rb><rt>di-go</rt><rb>盡頭</rb><rt>zing-deo</rt><rb>走來</rb><rt>tseo-læ</rt><rb>聽</rb><rt>tʽing</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>所羅門</rb><rt>Sô-lo-meng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>聰明個</rb><rt>tsʽong-ming-go</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>*shih-wô*</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>況且</rb><rt>hwông-tsʽia</rt><rb>蕩頭</rb><rt>dông-deo</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>比</rb><rt>pi</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>所羅門</rb><rt>Sô-lo-meng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>還</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>大個</rb><rt>do-go</rt><rb>來東</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt></ruby>。

<sup>43</sup> 「<ruby><rb>鏖糟</rb><rt>Ao-tsao</rt><rb>鬼</rb><rt>kyü</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>人個</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>身上</rb><rt>sing-zông</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>荒旱</rb><rt>hwông-ʽen</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>走來走去</rb><rt>tseo-læ-tseo-kyʽi</rt><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>尋</rb><rt>zing</rt><rb>安耽</rb><rt>en-tæn</rt><rb>烏蕩</rb><rt>*u-dông*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尋弗着</rb><rt>zing-feh-djôh</rt></ruby>。<sup>44</sup> <ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>keh-tsao</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>走轉</rb><rt>tseo-cün</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>前頭</rb><rt>zin-deo</rt><rb>走出個</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih-go</rt><rb>屋落</rb><rt>oh-lô</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>』」<ruby><rb>一</rb><rt>Ih</rt><rb>走到</rb><rt>tseo-tao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>壁淨清</rb><rt>pih-zing-tsʽing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>掃</rb><rt>sao</rt><rb>乾淨</rb><rt>ken-zing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>裝飾好了</rb><rt>tsông-sih-hao-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>45</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>Ziu</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>帶領</rb><rt>ta-ling</rt><rb>七個</rb><rt>tsʽih-go</rt><rb>鬼</rb><rt>kyü</rt><rb>比</rb><rt>pi</rt><rb>其自</rb><rt>gyi-zi</rt><rb>還</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>惡個</rb><rt>ôh-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>走進</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>箇頭</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>庉落</rb><rt>deng-lôh</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>結煞個</rb><rt>kyih-sah-go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>比</rb><rt>pi</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>起頭</rb><rt>kyʽi-deo</rt><rb>更加</rb><rt>keng-kô</rt><rb>壞了</rb><rt>w̆a-de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>現在個</rb><rt>Yin-dzæ-go</rt><rb>惡</rb><rt>ôh</rt><rb>世代</rb><rt>shü-dæ</rt><rb>將來</rb><rt>tsiang-læ</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是介個</rb><rt>z-ka-go</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>46</sup> 「<u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>YIÆ-SU</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>衆人</rb><rt>cong-nying</rt><rb>還</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>講個</rb><rt>kông-go</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>扣扣</rb><rt>kʽeo-kʽeo</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>阿孃</rb><rt>ah-nyiang</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>外頭</rb><rt>nga-deo</rt><rb>立間</rb><rt>lih-kæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要想</rb><rt>iao-siang</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt></ruby>。<sup>47</sup> <ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>且看</rb><rt>Tsʽia-kʽen</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾個</rb><rt>Ng-go</rt><rb>阿孃</rb><rt>ah-nyiang</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt><rb>外頭</rb><rt>nga-deo</rt><rb>立間</rb><rt>lih-kæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要想</rb><rt>iao-siang</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt></ruby>。」<sup>48</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>通知</rb><rt>tʽong-cü</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>阿孃</rb><rt>ah-nyiang</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>誰</rb><rt>jü</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>誰</rb><rt>jü</rt></ruby>？」<sup>49</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>Ziu</rt><rb>伸出</rb><rt>sing-cʽih</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>點點</rb><rt>tin-tin</rt><rb>門徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>且看</rb><rt>Tsʽia-kʽen</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>阿孃</rb><rt>ah-nyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt></ruby>！<sup>50</sup> <ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>依</rb><rt>i</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>天上</rb><rt>tʽin-zông</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>Ah-tia</rt><rb>旨意</rb><rt>ts-i</rt><rb>做個</rb><rt>tso-go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>姊妹</rb><rt>tsi-me</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>阿孃</rb><rt>ah-nyiang</rt></ruby>。」

---

### Mt. XIII.

<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>一日</rb><rt>ih-nyih</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>屋落</rb><rt>oh-lô</rt><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>坐</rb><rt>zo</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>海</rb><rt>hæ</rt><rb>邊沿</rb><rt>pin-yin</rt></ruby>。<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>一大潮</rb><rt>ih-do-dziao</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>走攏</rb><rt>tseo-long</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>烏碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>只</rb><rt>*tsih*</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>*hao*</rt><rb>跳落</rb><rt>tʽiao-lôh</rt><rb>船裏</rb><rt>jün-li</rt><rb>坐</rb><rt>zo</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>和潮</rb><rt>ʽo-dziao</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>立</rb><rt>lih</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>海塗上</rb><rt>hæ-du-zông</rt></ruby>。<sup>3</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>比方</rb><rt>pi-fông</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>許多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>聽</rb><rt>tʽing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：——

「<ruby><rb>且聽</rb><rt>TSʽIA-TʽING</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>扎</rb><rt>tsah</rt><rb>秧子</rb><rt>iang-ts</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt><rb>扎</rb><rt>tsah</rt><rb>秧子</rb><rt>iang-ts</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>扎開去</rb><rt>tsah-kʽæ-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有星</rb><rt>yiu-sing</rt><rb>跌落</rb><rt>tih-lôh</rt><rb>路</rb><rt>lu</rt><rb>邊沿</rb><rt>pin-yin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>鳥</rb><rt>tiao</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>飛來</rb><rt>fi-læ</rt><rb>喫掉了</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-diao-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>有星</rb><rt>Yiu-sing</rt><rb>跌落</rb><rt>tih-lôh</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>石頭</rb><rt>zah-deo</rt><rb>烏蕩</rb><rt>u-dông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爛泥</rb><rt>na-nyi</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>多</rb><rt>to</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>爛泥</rb><rt>na-nyi</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>深</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>緣故</rb><rt>yün-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>隨手</rb><rt>ze-siu</rt><rb>抽出來</rb><rt>tsʽiu-cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>；<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>日頭</rb><rt>nyih-deo</rt><rb>一</rb><rt>ih</rt><rb>出</rb><rt>cʽih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>曬癟</rb><rt>sa-pih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>根頭</rb><rt>keng-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>keh-lah</rt><rb>枯落了</rb><rt>kwʽu-lôh-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>有星</rb><rt>Yiu-sing</rt><rb>跌落</rb><rt>tih-lôh</rt><rb>刺棚</rb><rt>tsʽ-bang</rt><rb>縫裏</rb><rt>vong-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>刺</rb><rt>tsʽ</rt><rb>興起來</rb><rt>hying-kyʽi-læ</rt><rb>遮煞</rb><rt>tsô-sah</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>有星</rb><rt>Yiu-sing</rt><rb>跌落</rb><rt>tih-lôh</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>泥浴裏</rb><rt>nyi-nyüoh-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>結實</rb><rt>kyih-jih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有得個</rb><rt>yiu-teh-go</rt><rb>結</rb><rt>*kyih*</rt><rb>一百</rb><rt>ih-pah</rt><rb>倍</rb><rt>*be*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有得個</rb><rt>yiu-teh-go</rt><rb>六十</rb><rt>loh-jih</rt><rb>倍</rb><rt>*be*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有得個</rb><rt>yiu-teh-go</rt><rb>三十</rb><rt>sæn-jih</rt><rb>倍</rb><rt>be</rt></ruby>。<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>耳朵</rb><rt>ng-to</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>聽</rb><rt>tʽing</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>該</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>聽</rb><rt>tʽing</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>門徒</rb><rt>Meng-du</rt><rb>走攏去</rb><rt>tseo-long-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>聽</rb><rt>tʽing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>咋會</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>比方</rb><rt>pi-fông</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>？」<sup>11</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>天國個</rb><rt>tʽin-koh-go</rt><rb>奧妙</rb><rt>ao-miao</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>曉得個</rb><rt>*hyiao-teh-go*</rt></ruby>。<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>Væn-pah</rt><rb>有個</rb><rt>yiu-go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>還</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>加湊</rb><rt>kô-tsʽeo</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>有餘</rb><rt>yiu-yü</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>嘸得個</rb><rt>m̆-teh-go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>連</rb><rt>lin</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>有個</rb><rt>yiu-go</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>奪勒去</rb><rt>deh-leh-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>所以</rb><rt>sô-yi</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>比方</rb><rt>pi-fông</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>聽</rb><rt>tʽing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>看看</rb><rt>kʽen-kʽen</rt><rb>看弗出</rb><rt>kʽen-feh-cʽih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>聽聽</rb><rt>tʽing-tʽing</rt><rb>聽弗進</rb><rt>tʽing-feh-tsing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>明白</rb><rt>ming-bah</rt></ruby>。<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>Wa-yiu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以賽亞</rb><rt>Yi-sæ-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>預先</rb><rt>yü-sin</rt><rb>講個</rb><rt>kông-go</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>應着</rb><rt>ing-djôh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>身上</rb><rt>sing-zông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>聽</rb><rt>tʽing</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>聽聞其</rb><rt>tʽing-meng-gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>只是</rb><rt>tsih-z</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>看見其</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin-gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>只是</rb><rt>tsih-z</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>明白</rb><rt>ming-bah</rt></ruby>。<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>硬了</rb><rt>ngang-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>耳朵</rb><rt>ng-to</rt><rb>懶得</rb><rt>læn-teh</rt><rb>聽</rb><rt>tʽing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>眼睛</rb><rt>ngæn-tsing</rt><rb>閉的</rb><rt>pi-tih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>恐怕</rb><rt>kʽong-pʽô</rt><rb>眼睛</rb><rt>ngæn-tsing</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>耳朵</rb><rt>ng-to</rt><rb>聽聞</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>心裏</rb><rt>sing-li</rt><rb>明白</rb><rt>ming-bah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>回心轉意</rb><rt>we-sing-cün-i</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>醫</rb><rt>i</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt></ruby>。』 <sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt><rb>爾拉個</rb><rt>ng-lah-go</rt><rb>眼睛</rb><rt>ngæn-tsing</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>福氣</rb><rt>foh-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>看見個</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin-go</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>爾拉個</rb><rt>ng-lah-go</rt><rb>耳朵</rb><rt>ng-to</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>*yiu*</rt><rb>福氣</rb><rt>*foh-kyʽi*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>聽聞個</rb><rt>tʽing-meng-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>真</rb><rt>tsing</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>許多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>先知人</rb><rt>sin-cü-nying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>正直</rb><rt>tsing-dzih</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>要想</rb><rt>iao-siang</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>看見個</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>看見過</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin-ko</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>要想</rb><rt>*iao-siang*</rt><rb>聽</rb><rt>tʽing</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>聽聞個</rb><rt>tʽing-meng-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>聽聞過</rb><rt>tʽing-meng-ko</rt></ruby>。

<sup>18</sup> 「<ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>Ka-ni</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>扎</rb><rt>tsah</rt><rb>秧子</rb><rt>iang-ts</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>比方</rb><rt>pi-fông</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>聽</rb><rt>tʽing</rt></ruby>。<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>聽聞</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>天國個</rb><rt>*tʽin*-koh-go</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>明白個</rb><rt>ming-bah-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>惡個</rb><rt>ôh-go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>拔掉</rb><rt>bah-diao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>心裏</rb><rt>sing-li</rt><rb>扎的個</rb><rt>tsah-tih-go</rt><rb>秧子</rb><rt>iang-ts</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>扎</rb><rt>tsah</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>路</rb><rt>lu</rt><rb>邊沿</rb><rt>pin-yin</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>。<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>扎</rb><rt>Tsah</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>石頭</rb><rt>zah-deo</rt><rb>烏蕩</rb><rt>u-dông</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>聽聞</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>立刻</rb><rt>lih-kʽeh</rt><rb>歡歡喜喜</rb><rt>hwun-hwun-hyi-hyi</rt><rb>接受個</rb><rt>tsih-ziu-go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>。<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt><rb>心裏</rb><rt>sing-li</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>根頭</rb><rt>keng-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>不過</rb><rt>peh-ko</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>暫時個</rb><rt>dzæn-z-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt><rb>碰着</rb><rt>pʽong-djôh</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>委曲</rb><rt>w̆e-kyʽüoh</rt><rb>逼難個</rb><rt>pih-næn-go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>立刻</rb><rt>lih-kʽeh</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>𢲔跌</rb><rt>pæn-tih</rt></ruby>。<sup>22</sup> <ruby><rb>扎</rb><rt>Tsah</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>刺棚</rb><rt>tsʽ-bang</rt><rb>縫裏</rb><rt>vong-li</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>聽聞</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>今世個</rb><rt>kying-si-go</rt><rb>愁悶</rb><rt>zeo-meng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>財物個</rb><rt>dzæ-veh-go</rt><rb>哄騙</rb><rt>hong-pʽin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>遮住</rb><rt>tsô-djü</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>嘸陶成了</rb><rt>m-dao-dzing-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>23</sup> <ruby><rb>扎</rb><rt>Tsah</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>泥浴裏</rb><rt>nyi-nyüoh-li</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>聽聞</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>明白</rb><rt>ming-bah</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>倒</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>結實</rb><rt>kyih-jih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有得個</rb><rt>yiu-teh-go</rt><rb>結</rb><rt>kyih</rt><rb>一百</rb><rt>ih-pah</rt><rb>倍</rb><rt>*be*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有得個</rb><rt>yiu-teh-go</rt><rb>六十</rb><rt>loh-jih</rt><rb>倍</rb><rt>*be*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有得個</rb><rt>yiu-teh-go</rt><rb>三十</rb><rt>sæn-jih</rt><rb>倍</rb><rt>*be*</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>24</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>*YIÆ-SU*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>提起</rb><rt>di-kyʽi</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>比方</rb><rt>pi-fông</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>聽</rb><rt>tʽing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>天國</rb><rt>Tʽin-koh</rt><rb>可比</rb><rt>kʽo-pi</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>扎</rb><rt>tsah</rt><rb>好好</rb><rt>hao-hao</rt><rb>秧子</rb><rt>iang-ts</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>田裏</rb><rt>din-li</rt></ruby>。<sup>25</sup> <ruby><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>睏熟個</rb><rt>kwʽeng-joh-go</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>主人家個</rb><rt>cü-nying-kô-go</rt><rb>冤家</rb><rt>ün-kô</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>扎</rb><rt>tsah</rt><rb>稗草</rb><rt>bô-tsʽao</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>麥個</rb><rt>mah-go</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>去了</rb><rt>kyʽi-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>26</sup> <ruby><rb>等到</rb><rt>Teng-tao</rt><rb>麥</rb><rt>mah</rt><rb>抽苗</rb><rt>tsʽiu-miao</rt><rb>生佩個</rb><rt>sang-be-go</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>稗草</rb><rt>bô-tsʽao</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>看出了</rb><rt>kʽen-cʽih-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>27</sup> <ruby><rb>主人家個</rb><rt>Cü-nying-kô-go</rt><rb>奴僕</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>走來</rb><rt>tseo-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>！<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>啥</rb><rt>soh</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>秧子</rb><rt>iang-ts</rt><rb>扎</rb><rt>tsah</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>田裏</rb><rt>din-li</rt><rb>嗎</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>ka-ni</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>何裏來</rb><rt>ʽah-li-læ</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>稗草</rb><rt>bô-tsʽao</rt></ruby>？』<sup>28</sup> <ruby><rb>主人家</rb><rt>Cü-nying-kô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>冤家</rb><rt>ün-kô</rt><rb>做個</rb><rt>tso-go</rt></ruby>。』<ruby><rb>奴僕</rb><rt>Nu-boh</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>收拾</rb><rt>siu-jih</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt></ruby>？』<sup>29</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>好甮</rb><rt>Hao-vong</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>恐怕</rb><rt>kʽong-pʽô</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>收拾</rb><rt>siu-jih</rt><rb>稗草</rb><rt>bô-tsʽao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>連</rb><rt>lin</rt><rb>麥</rb><rt>mah</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>拔掉</rb><rt>bah-diao</rt></ruby>。<sup>30</sup> <ruby><rb>且</rb><rt>Tsʽia</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>兩樣</rb><rt>liang-yiang</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>大起來</rb><rt>do-kyʽi-læ</rt><rb>一直</rb><rt>ih-dzih</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>收割</rb><rt>siu-keh</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>收割個</rb><rt>Siu-keh-go</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>收割個</rb><rt>siu-keh-go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>先</rb><rt>sin</rt><rb>收拾</rb><rt>siu-jih</rt><rb>稗草</rb><rt>bô-tsʽao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>把打把</rb><rt>pô-tang-pô</rt><rb>縛帶攏</rb><rt>bo-tæn-long</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>燒</rb><rt>siao</rt><rb>火</rb><rt>ho</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt><rb>麥</rb><rt>mah</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>收進</rb><rt>siu-tsing</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>穀倉裏</rb><rt>koh-tsʽông-li</rt></ruby>。』」

<sup>31</sup> <ruby><rb>再</rb><rt>Tsæ</rt><rb>提起</rb><rt>di-kyʽi</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>比方</rb><rt>pi-fông</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>聽</rb><rt>tʽing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>天國</rb><rt>Tʽin-koh</rt><rb>可比像</rb><rt>kʽo-pi-ziang</rt><rb>一粒</rb><rt>ih-lih</rt><rb>芥菜子</rb><rt>ka-tsʽæ-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt><rb>馱勒</rb><rt>do-leh</rt><rb>下</rb><rt>ʽô</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>田裏</rb><rt>din-li</rt></ruby>。<sup>32</sup> <ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>Væn-pah</rt><rb>秧子</rb><rt>iang-ts</rt><rb>裏頭</rb><rt>li-deo</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>芥菜子</rb><rt>ka-tsʽæ-ts</rt><rb>頂</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>小</rb><rt>siao</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>長起來</rb><rt>dziang-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>菜蔬</rb><rt>tsʽæ-su</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>頂</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>成勒</rb><rt>dzing-leh</rt><rb>一株</rb><rt>ih-cü</rt><rb>樹</rb><rt>jü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>天裏個</rb><rt>tʽin-li-go</rt><rb>鳥</rb><rt>tiao</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>庉勒</rb><rt>deng-leh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>椏枝裏</rb><rt>ô-ts-li</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>33</sup> <ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>Yi</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>比方</rb><rt>pi-fông</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>聽</rb><rt>tʽing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>天國</rb><rt>Tʽin-koh</rt><rb>可比像</rb><rt>kʽo-pi-ziang</rt><rb>酵水</rb><rt>kao-shü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt><rb>馱來</rb><rt>do-læ</rt><rb>囥勒</rb><rt>kʽông-leh</rt><rb>三斗</rb><rt>sæn-teo</rt><rb>麪粉</rb><rt>min-feng</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等到</rb><rt>teng-tao</rt><rb>攏總</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>發酵</rb><rt>fah-kao</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>34</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>一切個</rb><rt>ih-tsʽih-go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>衆人</rb><rt>cong-nying</rt><rb>聽</rb><rt>tʽing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>比方</rb><rt>pi-fông</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>比方</rb><rt>pi-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt></ruby>。<sup>35</sup> <ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>先知人</rb><rt>sin-cü-nying</rt><rb>講過了</rb><rt>kông-ko-liao</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>效驗</rb><rt>yiao-nyiæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>比方</rb><rt>pi-fông</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>開</rb><rt>kʽæ</rt><rb>口</rb><rt>kʽeo</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>開闢</rb><rt>kʽæ-bih</rt><rb>天地</rb><rt>tʽin-di</rt><rb>以來</rb><rt>yi-læ</rt><rb>囥的個</rb><rt>kʽông-tih-go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>話出來</rb><rt>wô-cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>。」 

<sup>36</sup> <ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>KEH-TSAO</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>散開</rb><rt>sæn-kʽæ</rt><rb>箇大潮</rb><rt>keh-do-dziao</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>走進</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>屋落</rb><rt>oh-lô</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>門徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>走攏來</rb><rt>tseo-long-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>田裏</rb><rt>din-li</rt><rb>稗草個</rb><rt>bô-tsʽao-go</rt><rb>比方</rb><rt>pi-fông</rt><rb>話話</rb><rt>wô-wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>。」<sup>37</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>扎</rb><rt>Tsah</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>秧子</rb><rt>iang-ts</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>人個</rb><rt>Nying-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>Ng-ts</rt></ruby>。<sup>38</sup> <ruby><rb>田</rb><rt>din</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>世界</rb><rt>shü-ka</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>秧子</rb><rt>iang-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>天國個</rb><rt>tʽin-koh-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>稗草</rb><rt>bô-tsʽao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>惡個</rb><rt>ôh-go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby>。<sup>39</sup> <ruby><rb>扎</rb><rt>tsah</rt><rb>稗草</rb><rt>bô-tsʽao</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>冤家</rb><rt>ün-kô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>魔鬼</rb><rt>mo-kwe</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>收割</rb><rt>siu-keh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>時世</rb><rt>z-shü</rt><rb>末個</rb><rt>mah-go</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>收割個</rb><rt>siu-keh-go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>天使</rb><rt>tʽin-s</rt></ruby>。<sup>40</sup> <ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>Ka-ni</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>正像</rb><rt>tsing-ziang</rt><rb>稗草</rb><rt>bô-tsʽao</rt><rb>收拾攏</rb><rt>siu-jih-long</rt><rb>火裏</rb><rt>ho-li</rt><rb>燒掉</rb><rt>siao-diao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>時世</rb><rt>z-shü</rt><rb>末個</rb><rt>mah-go</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>是介個</rb><rt>z-ka-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>41</sup> <ruby><rb>人個</rb><rt>Nying-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>Ng-ts</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>天使</rb><rt>tʽin-s</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>國</rb><rt>koh</rt><rb>裏向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>收拾</rb><rt>siu-jih</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>帶累</rb><rt>ta-li</rt></ruby><sup> </sup><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>犯罪</rb><rt>væn-ze</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>非禮</rb><rt>fi-li</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>。<sup>42</sup> <ruby><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>丟</rb><rt>tiu</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>火爐裏</rb><rt>ho-lu-li</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>箇頭</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>咬勒</rb><rt>ngao-leh</rt><rb>牙齒</rb><rt>ngô-tsʽ</rt><rb>啼哭個</rb><rt>di-kʽoh-go</rt><rb>聲響</rb><rt>sing-hyiang</rt></ruby>。<sup>43</sup> <ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>正直</rb><rt>tsing-dzih</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>Ah-tia</rt><rb>國</rb><rt>koh</rt><rb>裏向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>日頭</rb><rt>nyih-deo</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>光亮</rb><rt>kwông-liang</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>耳朵</rb><rt>ng-to</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>聽</rb><rt>tʽing</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>該</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>聽</rb><rt>tʽing</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\]*ʽÔh-tsia* *wô*, s-teh nying pæn-tih. 或者話，使得人𢲔跌。（原註）</sup>

<sup>44</sup> 「<ruby><rb>天國</rb><rt>Tʽin-koh</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>可比像</rb><rt>kʽo-pi-ziang</rt><rb>田裏</rb><rt>din-li</rt><rb>囥的個</rb><rt>kʽông-tih-go</rt><rb>橫財</rb><rt>wang-dzæ</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>尋着</rb><rt>zing-djôh</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>蓋煞仔</rb><rt>kæ-sah-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>歡喜</rb><rt>hwun-hyi</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>歸去</rb><rt>kyü-kyʽi</rt><rb>賣掉</rb><rt>ma-diao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>攏總</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>家計</rb><rt>kô-kyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>買</rb><rt>ma</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>田</rb><rt>din</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。

<sup>45</sup> 「<ruby><rb>天國</rb><rt>Tʽin-koh</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>可比像</rb><rt>kʽo-pi-ziang</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>客商</rb><rt>kʽah-shông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>尋</rb><rt>zing</rt><rb>好個</rb><rt>hao-go</rt><rb>珠子</rb><rt>cü-ts</rt></ruby>。<sup>46</sup> <ruby><rb>尋着</rb><rt>zing-djôh</rt><rb>一粒</rb><rt>ih-lih</rt><rb>頂</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>值鈿個</rb><rt>dzih-din-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>歸去</rb><rt>kyü-kyʽi</rt><rb>賣掉</rb><rt>ma-diao</rt><rb>攏總</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>家計</rb><rt>kô-kyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>買</rb><rt>ma</rt><rb>箇粒</rb><rt>keh-lih</rt><rb>珠子</rb><rt>cü-ts</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。

<sup>47</sup> 「<ruby><rb>天國</rb><rt>Tʽin-koh</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>可比像</rb><rt>kʽo-pi-ziang</rt><rb>一頂</rb><rt>ih-ting</rt><rb>網</rb><rt>mông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>下</rb><rt>ʽô</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>海裏</rb><rt>hæ-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>兜進</rb><rt>teo-tsing</rt><rb>百樣</rb><rt>pah-yiang</rt><rb>魚</rb><rt>*ng*</rt></ruby>。<sup>48</sup> <ruby><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>滿了</rb><rt>mun-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>拖上</rb><rt>tʽo-zông</rt><rb>岸</rb><rt>ngen</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>坐落</rb><rt>zo-lôh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好個</rb><rt>hao-go</rt><rb>揀進</rb><rt>kæn-tsing</rt><rb>水桶裏</rb><rt>shü-dong-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>壞個</rb><rt>w̆a-go</rt><rb>丟掉</rb><rt>tiu-diao</rt></ruby>。<sup>49</sup> <ruby><rb>時世</rb><rt>Z-shü</rt><rb>末個</rb><rt>mah-go</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>天使</rb><rt>tʽin-s</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>出來</rb><rt>cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>正直</rb><rt>tsing-dzih</rt><rb>人個</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>分出</rb><rt>feng-cʽih</rt><rb>惡人</rb><rt>ôh-nying</rt></ruby>，<sup>50</sup> <ruby><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>丟勒</rb><rt>tiu-leh</rt><rb>火爐裏</rb><rt>ho-lu-li</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>箇頭</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>咬勒牙齒</rb><rt>ngao-leh-ngô-tsʽ</rt><rb>啼哭個</rb><rt>di-kʽoh-go</rt><rb>聲響</rb><rt>sing-hyiang</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>51</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>攏總</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>*shih-wô*</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>明白</rb><rt>ming-bah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt></ruby>？」<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>明白個</rb><rt>ming-bah-go</rt></ruby>。」<sup>52</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>所以</rb><rt>Sô-yi</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>讀書人</rb><rt>doh-shü-nying</rt><rb>天國個</rb><rt>tʽin-koh-go</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt><rb>學會了</rb><rt>ʽôh-we-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>可比像</rb><rt>kʽo-pi-ziang</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>家主</rb><rt>kô-cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>倉庫</rb><rt>tsʽông-kwʽu</rt><rb>裏向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>新</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>東西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt><rb>舊</rb><rt>gyiu</rt><rb>東西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>馱出來</rb><rt>do-cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>53</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>YIÆ-SU</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>講好</rb><rt>kông-hao</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>比方</rb><rt>pi-fông</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>離開</rb><rt>li-kʽæ</rt><rb>箇頭</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt></ruby>。<sup>54</sup> <ruby><rb>走</rb><rt>tseo</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>自個</rb><rt>Zi-go</rt><rb>本</rb><rt>peng</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>聚會堂裏</rb><rt>jü-we-dông-li</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>聽</rb><rt>tʽing</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>喫𢞕</rb><rt>kyʽih-hoh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>Nying</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>何裏</rb><rt>ʽah-li</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>聰明</rb><rt>tsʽong-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>神蹟</rb><rt>jing-tsih</rt></ruby>？<sup>55</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>豈</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>木匠</rb><rt>moh-ziang</rt><rb>師父個</rb><rt>s-vu-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>Ng-ts</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>阿孃</rb><rt>ah-nyiang</rt><rb>豈</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>馬利亞</rb><rt>Mô-li-üô</rt></ruby></u>？<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt><rb>豈</rb><rt>*kyʽi*</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>*feh-z*</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>*kyiao-leh*</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>雅各</rb><rt>Yüô-kôh</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>約西</rb><rt>Iah-si</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>西門</rb><rt>Si-meng</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>猶大</rb><rt>Yiu-da</rt></ruby></u>？<sup>56</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>姊妹</rb><rt>tsi-me</rt><rb>豈</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>攏總</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>住東</rb><rt>djü-tong</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>Ka-ni</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>何裏</rb><rt>ʽah-li</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>攏總</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>？」<sup>57</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>Ziu</rt><rb>厭憎</rb><rt>in-tseng</rt><rb>棄掉</rb><rt>kyʽi-diao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>先知人</rb><rt>Sin-cü-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>其自個</rb><rt>gyi-zi-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>自個</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>屋落</rb><rt>oh-lô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>再𣍐</rb><rt>tsæ-ve</rt><rb>嘸體面個</rb><rt>m-tʽi-min-go</rt></ruby>。」<sup>58</sup> <ruby><rb>爲了</rb><rt>We-leh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>緣故</rb><rt>yün-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>箇頭</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m̆-teh</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>多少</rb><rt>to-siao</rt><rb>神蹟</rb><rt>jing-tsih</rt></ruby>。

### Mt. XIV.

<ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>KEH-GO</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>藩王</rb><rt>væn-wông</rt></ruby><sup> </sup><u><ruby><rb>希律</rb><rt>Hyi-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>聽聞</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>耶穌</u>個</rb><rt>Yiæ-su-go</rt><rb>名聲</rb><rt>ming-sing</rt></ruby>，<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>臣子</rb><rt>dzing-ts</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>行洗禮個</rb><rt>ʽAng-si-li-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>約翰</rb><rt>Iah-ʽen</rt></ruby></u>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>死人</rb><rt>si-nying</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>活轉來個</rb><rt>weh-cün-læ-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>keh-lah</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>顯出</rb><rt>hyin-cʽih</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>神蹟</rb><rt>jing-tsih</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。」

（<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>希律</rb><rt>Hyi-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>前頭</rb><rt>*zin-deo*</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt></ruby><sup> </sup><ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>腓力</u>個</rb><rt>Fi-lih-go</rt><rb>老𡢿</rb><rt>lao-nyüing</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>希羅底</u>個</rb><rt>Hyi-lo-ti-go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>抲</rb><rt>kʽô</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>約翰</rb><rt>Iah-ʽen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>縛仔</rb><rt>bo-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>關</rb><rt>kwæn</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>牢監裏</rb><rt>lao-kæn-li</rt></ruby>。<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>約翰</rb><rt>Iah-ʽen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>希律</rb><rt>Hyi-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>應該</rb><rt>ing-kæ</rt><rb>娶</rb><rt>cʽü</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。」<sup>5</sup> <u><ruby><rb>希律</rb><rt>*Hyi-lih*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>打賬</rb><rt>tang-tsiang</rt><rb>殺</rb><rt>sah</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>約翰</rb><rt>Iah-ʽen</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>只是</rb><rt>*tsih-z*</rt><rb>怕</rb><rt>pʽô</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>算</rb><rt>sön</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>先知人</rb><rt>sin-cü-nying</rt></ruby>。<sup>6</sup> <u><ruby><rb>希律</rb><rt>Hyi-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>做生</rb><rt>tso-sang</rt></ruby><sup> </sup><ruby><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb><u>希羅底</u>個</rb><rt>Hyi-lo-ti-go</rt><rb>囡</rb><rt>nön</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>當面前</rb><rt>tông-min-zin</rt></ruby><sup> </sup><ruby><rb>跳舞</rb><rt>tʽiao-vu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>希律</rb><rt>Hyi-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>歡喜</rb><rt>hwun-hyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>Keh-lah</rt><rb>罰咒</rb><rt>vah-tsiu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>應許</rb><rt>ing-hyü</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>娘子</rb><rt>nyiang-ts</rt><rb>隨便</rb><rt>ze-bin</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>啥個</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>東西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt></ruby>。<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>娘子</rb><rt>Nyiang-ts</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>阿孃</rb><rt>ah-nyiang</rt><rb>挑唆</rb><rt>tʽiao-so</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>行洗禮</rb><rt>ʽAng-si-li</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>約翰</u>個</rb><rt>Iah-ʽen-go</rt><rb>頭</rb><rt>deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>盤裏</rb><rt>bun-li</rt><rb>𣥼仔</rb><rt>tsi-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>蕩頭</rb><rt>dông-deo</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby>。」<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>國王</rb><rt>Koh-wông</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>憂悶</rb><rt>iu-meng</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>只是</rb><rt>tsih-z</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>罰過咒</rb><rt>vah-ko-tsiu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>同席個</rb><rt>dong-zih-go</rt><rb>人客</rb><rt>nying-kʽah</rt><rb>來東</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>keh-lah</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>Ziu</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>牢監裏</rb><rt>lao-kæn-li</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>斬</rb><rt>tsæn</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>約翰</u>個</rb><rt>Iah-ʽen-go</rt><rb>頭</rb><rt>deo</rt></ruby>。<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>其個</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>頭</rb><rt>deo</rt><rb>盤裏</rb><rt>bun-li</rt><rb>𣥼仔</rb><rt>tsi-ts</rt><rb>馱來</rb><rt>do-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>交付</rb><rt>kao-fu</rt><rb>娘子</rb><rt>nyiang-ts</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>娘子</rb><rt>*nyiang-ts*</rt><rb>馱去</rb><rt>do-kyʽi</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>阿孃</rb><rt>ah-nyiang</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb><u>約翰</u>個</rb><rt>Iah-ʽen-go</rt><rb>門徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>走來</rb><rt>tseo-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>將</rb><rt>tsiang</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>屍首</rb><rt>s-siu</rt><rb>安葬好</rb><rt>en-tsông-hao</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>告訴</rb><rt>kao-su</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u>。）

<sup>13</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>聽聞</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>離開</rb><rt>li-kʽæ</rt><rb>箇頭</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>落</rb><rt>lôh</rt><rb>船</rb><rt>jün</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>私下</rb><rt>s-ʽô</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>一𡍲</rb><rt>ih-tʽah</rt><rb>曠野</rb><rt>kwʽông-iæ</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>一大潮</rb><rt>ih-do-dziao</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>得知</rb><rt>teh-cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>各</rb><rt>kôh</rt><rb>城裏</rb><rt>zing-li</rt><rb>步行</rb><rt>bu-ʽang</rt><rb>跟勒</rb><rt>keng-leh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>14</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>走出來</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一大潮</rb><rt>ih-do-dziao</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>哀憐</rb><rt>æ-lin</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>醫好</rb><rt>i-hao</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>有病個</rb><rt>yiu-bing-go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>。

<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>Tao</rt><rb>夜快</rb><rt>yia-kwʽa</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>門徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>走攏來</rb><rt>tseo-long-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>蕩頭</rb><rt>Dông-deo</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>曠野</rb><rt>kwʽông-iæ</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>遲了</rb><rt>dzi-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>散開</rb><rt>sæn-kʽæ</rt><rb>箇大潮</rb><rt>keh-do-dziao</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>鄉村</rb><rt>hyiang-tsʽeng</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>買</rb><rt>ma</rt><rb>喫食</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-zih</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。」<sup>16</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>門徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>不必</rb><rt>peh-pih</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>喫</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt></ruby>。」<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>門徒</rb><rt>Meng-du</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>蕩頭</rb><rt>dông-deo</rt><rb>只</rb><rt>tsih</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>五個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>饅頭</rb><rt>mun-deo</rt><rb>兩梗</rb><rt>liang-kwang</rt><rb>魚</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>來東</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt></ruby>。」<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>馱來</rb><rt>Do-læ</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>。」<sup>19</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>箇大潮</rb><rt>keh-do-dziao</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>青草</rb><rt>tsʽing-tsʽao</rt><rb>地上</rb><rt>di-zông</rt><rb>坐帶落</rb><rt>zo-tæn-lôh</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>馱勒</rb><rt>do-leh</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>五個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>饅頭</rb><rt>mun-deo</rt><rb>兩梗</rb><rt>liang-kwang</rt><rb>魚</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>頭</rb><rt>deo</rt><rb>抬起</rb><rt>dæ-kyʽi</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>天</rb><rt>tʽin</rt><rb>懺唸記</rb><rt>tsʽæn-nyiæn-kyi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>𤖼開</rb><rt>pʽah-kʽæ</rt><rb>饅頭</rb><rt>mun-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>交付</rb><rt>kao-fu</rt><rb>門徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>門徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>分</rb><rt>*feng*</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>*peh*</rt><rb>箇大潮</rb><rt>keh-do-dziao</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>攏總</rb><rt>Long-tsong</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>喫過</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-ko</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>喫飽了</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-pao-de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>喫剩個</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-dzing-go</rt><rb>零碎</rb><rt>ling-se</rt><rb>收拾攏</rb><rt>siu-jih-long</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>滿滿</rb><rt>mun-mun</rt><rb>十二</rb><rt>jih-nyi</rt><rb>籃</rb><rt>læn</rt></ruby>。<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>喫個</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>男</rb><rt>nen</rt><rb>約來</rb><rt>iah-læ</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>五千</rb><rt>ng-tsʽin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt><rb>小人</rb><rt>siao-nying</rt><rb>在外</rb><rt>dzæ-nga</rt></ruby>。

<sup>22</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>隨手</rb><rt>ze-siu</rt><rb>催</rb><rt>tsʽe</rt><rb>門徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>落</rb><rt>lôh</rt><rb>船</rb><rt>jün</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>先</rb><rt>sin</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>對岸</rb><rt>te-ngen</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>自</rb><rt>*Zi*</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>*iao*</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>*teng*</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>散開</rb><rt>sæn-kʽæ</rt><rb>箇大潮</rb><rt>keh-do-dziao</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。<sup>23</sup> <ruby><rb>箇大潮</rb><rt>Keh-do-dziao</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>散開仔</rb><rt>sæn-kʽæ-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>走上</rb><rt>tseo-zông</rt><rb>山頂</rb><rt>sæn-teng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>私下</rb><rt>s-ʽô</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>禱告</rb><rt>tao-kao</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>已經</rb><rt>Yi-kying</rt><rb>夜快</rb><rt>yia-kwʽa</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>獨自</rb><rt>doh-zi</rt><rb>來間</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt></ruby>。<sup>24</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt><rb>船</rb><rt>jün</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>海</rb><rt>hæ</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>來間</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>浪</rb><rt>lông</rt><rb>盪來盪去</rb><rt>dông-læ-dông-kyʽi</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>鬥風</rb><rt>teo-fong</rt><rb>緣故</rb><rt>yün-kwu</rt></ruby>。

<sup>25</sup> <ruby><rb>當夜</rb><rt>Tông-yia</rt><rb>四更</rb><rt>s-kang</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>門徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>烏蕩</rb><rt>u-dông</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>海</rb><rt>hæ</rt><rb>面頂</rb><rt>min-teng</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>走勒來</rb><rt>tseo-leh-læ</rt></ruby>。<sup>26</sup> <ruby><rb>門徒</rb><rt>Meng-du</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>海</rb><rt>hæ</rt><rb>面頂</rb><rt>min-teng</rt><rb>來間</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt><rb>走</rb><rt>tseo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>喫𢞕</rb><rt>kyʽih-hoh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>顯靈</rb><rt>hyin-ling</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>怕</rb><rt>pʽô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>懷</rb><rt>wæ</rt><rb>一聲</rb><rt>ih-sing</rt><rb>謳起來</rb><rt>eo-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>。<sup>27</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>立刻</rb><rt>lih-kʽeh</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>一聲</rb><rt>ih-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>放心</rb><rt>Fông-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好甮</rb><rt>hao-vong</rt><rb>怕</rb><rt>pʽô</rt></ruby>。」<sup>28</sup> <u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>謳</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>面頂</rb><rt>min-teng</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>烏碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。」<sup>29</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>Hao</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。」<u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>船裏</rb><rt>jün-li</rt><rb>跳落</rb><rt>tʽiao-lôh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>面頂</rb><rt>min-teng</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>走</rb><rt>tseo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>烏碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>30</sup> <ruby><rb>看見</rb><rt>Kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>風</rb><rt>fong</rt><rb>猛</rb><rt>mang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>怕起來了</rb><rt>pʽô-kyʽi-læ-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>沉落去</rb><rt>dzing-lôh-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>懷聲</rb><rt>wæ-sing</rt><rb>謳起來</rb><rt>eo-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>救</rb><rt>kyiu</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby>。」<sup>31</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>立刻</rb><rt>lih-kʽeh</rt><rb>伸出</rb><rt>sing-cʽih</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>揸</rb><rt>cô</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>小</rb><rt>siao</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>咋會</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>疑心</rb><rt>nyi-sing</rt></ruby>？」<sup>32</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>走進</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>船裏</rb><rt>jün-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>風</rb><rt>fong</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>息了</rb><rt>sih-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>33</sup> <ruby><rb>船裏個</rb><rt>Jün-li-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>拜</rb><rt>pa</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>真真</rb><rt>tsing-tsing</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>Ng-ts</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>34</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>渡過</rb><rt>du-ko</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>革尼撒勒</rb><rt>Keh-nyi-sah-leh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>。<sup>35</sup> <ruby><rb>箇𡍲</rb><rt>Keh-tʽah</rt><rb>地方個</rb><rt>di-fông-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>認得</rb><rt>nying-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>四凸亂圈</rb><rt>s-deh-lön-kyʽün</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>通知</rb><rt>*tʽong-cü*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>攏總</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>有病個</rb><rt>yiu-bing-go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>帶</rb><rt>ta</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>烏碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>來了</rb><rt>læ-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>36</sup> <ruby><rb>求求</rb><rt>gyiu-gyiu</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>單只</rb><rt>tæn-tsih</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>按按</rb><rt>en-en</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>衣裳</rb><rt>i-zông</rt><rb>衦頭</rb><rt>ken-den</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>按個</rb><rt>en-go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>好了</rb><rt>hao-de</rt></ruby>。

---

### Mt. XV.

<ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>KEH-GO</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>耶路撒冷</u>個</rb><rt>Yiæ-lu-sah-leng-go</rt><rb>讀書人</rb><rt>doh-shü-nying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法利賽</rb><rt>Fah-li-sæ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>走</rb><rt>tseo</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>烏碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：<sup>2</sup> 「<ruby><rb>爾個</rb><rt>Ng-go</rt><rb>門徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>啥</rb><rt>soh</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>違背</rb><rt>we-be</rt><rb>祖宗</rb><rt>tsu-tsong</rt><rb>傳落來個</rb><rt>djün-lôh-læ-go</rt><rb>古典</rb><rt>kwu-tin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>喫飯</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-væn</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>作</rb><rt>tsôh</rt><rb>滰</rb><rt>gyiang</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt></ruby>？」<sup>3</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>啥</rb><rt>soh</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>傳落來個</rb><rt>djün-lôh-læ-go</rt><rb>古典</rb><rt>kwu-tin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>違背</rb><rt>we-be</rt><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>Yüong</rt><rb>敬重</rb><rt>kying-djong</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>父母</rb><rt>vu-meo</rt></ruby>；』 <ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>𧮙罵</rb><rt>Zôh-mô</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>ah-tia</rt><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>阿孃</rb><rt>ah-nyiang</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>必</rb><rt>pih</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>辦</rb><rt>bæn</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>死罪</rb><rt>si-ze</rt></ruby>。』 <sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>Ziah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>ah-tia</rt><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>阿孃</rb><rt>ah-nyiang</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>應當</rb><rt>ing-tông</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>好處</rb><rt>hao-cʽü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>尊起</rb><rt>tseng-kyʽi</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>*peh*</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>*Jing-ming*</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>*de*</rt></ruby>。<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>好甮</rb><rt>hao-vong</rt><rb>敬重</rb><rt>kying-djong</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>ah-tia</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>阿孃</rb><rt>ah-nyiang</rt></ruby>。』<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>Z-ka</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>傳落來個</rb><rt>djün-lôh-læ-go</rt><rb>古典</rb><rt>kwu-tin</rt><rb>廢掉</rb><rt>fi-diao</rt><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt></ruby>。<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>假好人</rb><rt>Kô-hao-nying</rt></ruby>！<u><ruby><rb>以賽亞</rb><rt>Yi-sæ-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>指點</rb><rt>ts-tin</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>預先</rb><rt>yü-sin</rt><rb>講個</rb><rt>kông-go</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>實在</rb><rt>jih-dzæ</rt><rb>是個</rb><rt>z-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：<sup>8</sup> 『<ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>口裏</rb><rt>kʽeo-li</rt><rb>親近</rb><rt>tsʽing-gying</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>嘴巴</rb><rt>cü-pô</rt><rb>敬重</rb><rt>kying-djong</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>只是</rb><rt>tsih-z</rt><rb>其拉個</rb><rt>gyi-lah-go</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>離遠</rb><rt>li-yün</rt></ruby>。<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>人個</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>當</rb><rt>tông</rt><rb>神明個</rb><rt>*Jing-ming-go*</rt><rb>教訓</rb><rt>kao-hyüing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>白力力</rb><rt>bah-lih-lih</rt><rb>服侍</rb><rt>voh-z</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>。』 」

<sup>10</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>謳攏</rb><rt>eo-long</rt><rb>箇潮</rb><rt>keh-dziao</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>該</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>聽</rb><rt>tʽing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>明白</rb><rt>ming-bah</rt></ruby>。<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>走進</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>嘴巴裏</rb><rt>cü-pô-li</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>東西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>弄</rb><rt>long</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>鏖糟</rb><rt>ao-tsao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>只有</rb><rt>tsih-yiu</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>嘴巴裏</rb><rt>cü-pô-li</rt><rb>出來個</rb><rt>cʽih-læ-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>弄</rb><rt>long</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>鏖糟</rb><rt>ao-tsao</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>Keh-tsao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>門徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>走攏來</rb><rt>tseo-long-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<u><ruby><rb>法利賽</rb><rt>Fah-li-sæ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>聽聞</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>箇句</rb><rt>keh-kyü</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>怏了</rb><rt>ông-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>得知</rb><rt>teh-cü</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt></ruby>？」<sup>13</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>Væn-pah</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>天上</rb><rt>tʽin-zông</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>Ah-tia</rt><rb>種的個</rb><rt>cong-tih-go</rt><rb>樹</rb><rt>jü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>總</rb><rt>tsong</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>連</rb><rt>lin</rt><rb>根</rb><rt>keng</rt><rb>拔掉</rb><rt>bah-diao</rt></ruby>。<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>甮</rb><rt>vong</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>管</rb><rt>kwun</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>瞎子</rb><rt>hah-ts</rt><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>領</rb><rt>ling</rt><rb>瞎子個</rb><rt>hah-ts-go</rt><rb>路</rb><rt>lu</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>Ziah-z</rt><rb>瞎子</rb><rt>hah-ts</rt><rb>領</rb><rt>ling</rt><rb>瞎子</rb><rt>hah-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>兩下</rb><rt>liang-ʽô</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>跌落</rb><rt>tih-lôh</rt><rb>窞裏</rb><rt>den-li</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>15</sup> <u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>開</rb><rt>kʽæ</rt><rb>口</rb><rt>kʽeo</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>比方</rb><rt>pi-fông</rt><rb>話話</rb><rt>wô-wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>。」<sup>16</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>連</rb><rt>Lin</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>如今</rb><rt>jü-kying</rt><rb>還</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>明白</rb><rt>ming-bah</rt></ruby>！<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>啥</rb><rt>Soh</rt><rb>還</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>走進</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>嘴巴裏</rb><rt>cü-pô-li</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>東西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>行落</rb><rt>ʽang-lôh</rt><rb>肚皮</rb><rt>du-bi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>介出</rb><rt>ka-cʽih</rt><rb>坑子裏</rb><rt>kʽang-ts-li</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>？<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt><rb>嘴巴裏</rb><rt>cü-pô-li</rt><rb>走出來個</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih-læ-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>心裏</rb><rt>sing-li</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>發出來</rb><rt>fah-cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>弄</rb><rt>long</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>鏖糟</rb><rt>ao-tsao</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>。<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>惡個</rb><rt>ôh-go</rt><rb>忖頭</rb><rt>tsʽeng-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>兇手</rb><rt>hyüong-siu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>偷</rb><rt>tʽeo</rt><rb>老𡢿</rb><rt>lao-nyüing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>嫖</rb><rt>biao</rt><rb>婊子</rb><rt>piao-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>盜賊</rb><rt>dao-zeh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>假</rb><rt>kô</rt><rb>對證</rb><rt>te-tsing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>謗毀</rb><rt>pông-hwe</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>心裏</rb><rt>sing-li</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>發出來</rb><rt>fah-cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>。<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>弄</rb><rt>long</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>鏖糟</rb><rt>ao-tsao</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>滰</rb><rt>gyiang</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>喫飯</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-væn</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>弄</rb><rt>long</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>鏖糟</rb><rt>ao-tsao</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>21</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>YIÆ-SU</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>離開</rb><rt>li-kʽæ</rt><rb>箇頭</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>趲</rb><rt>tsæn</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>推羅</rb><rt>Tʽe-lo</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>西頓</rb><rt>Si-teng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>相近個</rb><rt>siang-gying-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>。<sup>22</sup> <ruby><rb>扣扣</rb><rt>Kʽeo-kʽeo</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>迦南個</rb><rt>Kyüô-nen-go</rt><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>地界</rb><rt>di-ka</rt><rb>走出來</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>響響</rb><rt>hyiang-hyiang</rt><rb>謳</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb><u>大闢</u>個</rb><rt>Da-bih-go</rt><rb>後代</rb><rt>ʽEo-dæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>哀憐</rb><rt>æ-lin</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>我個</rb><rt>ngô-go</rt><rb>囡</rb><rt>nön</rt><rb>邪病</rb><rt>zia-bing</rt><rb>生勒</rb><rt>sang-leh</rt><rb>厲害個</rb><rt>li-ʽæ-go</rt></ruby>。」<sup>23</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>一句</rb><rt>ih-kyü</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>門徒</rb><rt>Meng-du</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>走攏來</rb><rt>tseo-long-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>請問</rb><rt>tsʽing-meng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>Hao</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>後背</rb><rt>ʽeo-pe</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>懷懷</rb><rt>wæ-wæ</rt><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>響</rb><rt>hyiang</rt></ruby>。」<sup>24</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>單只</rb><rt>tæn-tsih</rt><rb>奉差</rb><rt>vong-tsʽa</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>箇族</rb><rt>keh-dzoh</rt><rb>失掉間個</rb><rt>shih-diao-kæn-go</rt><rb>羊</rb><rt>yiang</rt><rb>烏蕩</rb><rt>u-dông</rt><rb>來個</rb><rt>læ-go</rt></ruby>。」<sup>25</sup> <ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt><rb>走攏</rb><rt>tseo-long</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>拜拜</rb><rt>pa-pa</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>幫助</rb><rt>pông-dzu</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby>。」<sup>26</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>*gyi*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>M-neh</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>馱</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>兒囡個</rb><rt>ng-nô-go</rt><rb>喫食</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-zih</rt><rb>丟</rb><rt>tiu</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>黃狗</rb><rt>wun-kyi</rt></ruby>。」<sup>27</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是個</rb><rt>z-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>黃狗</rb><rt>wun-kyi</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>喫</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt><rb>主人家</rb><rt>cü-nying-kô</rt><rb>桌凳下</rb><rt>coh-teng-ʽô</rt><rb>跌落個</rb><rt>tih-lôh-go</rt><rb>零碎</rb><rt>ling-se</rt></ruby>。」<sup>28</sup> <ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>Keh-tsao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>女人</rb><rt>Nyü-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>相信個</rb><rt>siang-sing-go</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>大個</rb><rt>do-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>依</rb><rt>i</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>心願</rb><rt>sing-nyün</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>其個</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>囡</rb><rt>nön</rt><rb>當時</rb><rt>tông-z</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>好了</rb><rt>hao-de</rt></ruby>。

<sup>29</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>YIÆ-SU</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>離開</rb><rt>li-kʽæ</rt><rb>箇頭</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>打</rb><rt>tang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>加利利</rb><rt>Kyüô-li-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>海</rb><rt>hæ</rt><rb>邊沿</rb><rt>pin-yin</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>走上</rb><rt>tseo-zông</rt><rb>一座</rb><rt>ih-zo</rt><rb>山頂</rb><rt>sæn-teng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>坐間</rb><rt>zo-kæn</rt></ruby>。<sup>30</sup> <ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>一大潮</rb><rt>ih-do-dziao</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>帶領</rb><rt>ta-ling</rt><rb>拐腳</rb><rt>kwa-kyiah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>瞎眼</rb><rt>hah-ngæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>啞子</rb><rt>ô-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>殘缺不全個</rb><rt>dzæn-kyʽüih-peh-djün-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>許多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>別個</rb><rt>bih-go</rt><rb>病人</rb><rt>*bing-nying*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>走攏</rb><rt>tseo-long</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>烏碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>放</rb><rt>fông</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>腳下</rb><rt>kyiah-ʽô</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>醫好了</rb><rt>i-hao-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>31</sup> <ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>箇大潮</rb><rt>keh-do-dziao</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>啞子</rb><rt>ô-ts</rt><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>殘缺個</rb><rt>dzæn-kyʽüih-go</rt><rb>完全</rb><rt>wun-djün</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>拐腳個</rb><rt>kwa-kyiah-go</rt><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>走</rb><rt>tseo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>瞎眼</rb><rt>hah-ngæn</rt><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>稀奇</rb><rt>hyi-gyi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>讚美</rb><rt>tsæn-me</rt><rb>箇位</rb><rt>keh-we</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>以色列</u>個</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>。

<sup>32</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>謳攏</rb><rt>eo-long</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>門徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>哀憐</rb><rt>æ-lin</rt><rb>箇潮</rb><rt>keh-dziao</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>來東</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>三</rb><rt>sæn</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>難間</rb><rt>*næn-kæn*</rt><rb>嘸告</rb><rt>m-kao</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>喫</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>餓勒</rb><rt>ngo-leh</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>恐怕</rb><rt>kʽong-pʽô</rt><rb>路裏</rb><rt>lu-li</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>脫力</rb><rt>tʽeh-lih</rt></ruby>。」<sup>33</sup> <ruby><rb>門徒</rb><rt>Meng-du</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>曠野</rb><rt>kwʽông-iæ</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>何裏來</rb><rt>ʽah-li-læ</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>箇打</rb><rt>keh-tang</rt><rb>喫食</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-zih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>一大潮</rb><rt>ih-do-dziao</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>喫飽</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-pao</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？」<sup>34</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>幾個</rb><rt>kyi-go</rt><rb>饅頭</rb><rt>mun-deo</rt><rb>來東</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt></ruby>？」<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>七個</rb><rt>Tsʽih-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>幾梗</rb><rt>kyi-kwang</rt><rb>小小</rb><rt>siao-siao</rt><rb>魚</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>。」<sup>35</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>箇潮</rb><rt>keh-dziao</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>地垟裏</rb><rt>di-yiang-li</rt><rb>坐帶落</rb><rt>zo-tæn-lôh</rt></ruby>。<sup>36</sup> <ruby><rb>馱勒</rb><rt>do-leh</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>七個</rb><rt>tsʽih-go</rt><rb>饅頭</rb><rt>mun-deo</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>魚</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>謝好仔</rb><rt>zia-hao-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>𤖼開</rb><rt>pʽah-kʽæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>交付</rb><rt>kao-fu</rt><rb>門徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>門徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>分</rb><rt>*feng*</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>*peh*</rt><rb>箇潮</rb><rt>keh-dziao</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。<sup>37</sup> <ruby><rb>攏總</rb><rt>Long-tsong</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>喫過</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-ko</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>喫飽了</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-pao-de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>收拾</rb><rt>siu-jih</rt><rb>喫剩個</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-dzing-go</rt><rb>零碎</rb><rt>ling-se</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>滿滿</rb><rt>mun-mun</rt><rb>七</rb><rt>tsʽih</rt><rb>籃</rb><rt>læn</rt></ruby>。<sup>38</sup> <ruby><rb>喫個</rb><rt>Kyʽüoh-go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>男</rb><rt>nen</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>四千</rb><rt>s-tsʽin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt><rb>小人</rb><rt>siao-nying</rt><rb>在外</rb><rt>dzæ-nga</rt></ruby>。

<sup>39</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>散開</rb><rt>sæn-kʽæ</rt><rb>箇大潮</rb><rt>keh-do-dziao</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>落</rb><rt>lôh</rt><rb>船</rb><rt>jün</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>抹大拉</u>個</rb><rt>Meh-da-lah-go</rt><rb>地界</rb><rt>di-ka</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。
