### Mt. XX.

「<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>天國</rb><rt>tʽin-koh</rt><rb>可比像</rb><rt>kʽo-pi-ziang</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>家主</rb><rt>kô-cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>早</rb><rt>tsao</rt><rb>天亮</rb><rt>tʽin-nyiang</rt><rb>走出去</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih-kyʽi</rt><rb>僱</rb><rt>kwu</rt><rb>工人</rb><rt>kong-nying</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>葡萄園裏</rb><rt>bu-dao-yün-li</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>Teng</rt><rb>工人</rb><rt>kong-nying</rt><rb>講開</rb><rt>kông-kʽæ</rt><rb>一鈿</rb><rt>ih-din</rt><rb>銀子</rb><rt>nying-ts</rt><rb>一日</rb><rt>ih-nyih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>謳</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>葡萄園裏</rb><rt>bu-dao-yün-li</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>毛</rb><rt>Mao</rt><rb>九</rb><rt>kyiu</rt><rb>點鐘</rb><rt>tin-cong</rt><rb>走出去</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>別人</rb><rt>bih-nying</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>市面上</rb><rt>z-min-zông</rt><rb>空頭</rb><rt>kʽong-deo</rt><rb>立間</rb><rt>lih-kæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：<sup>4</sup> 『<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>葡萄園裏</rb><rt>bu-dao-yün-li</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>平直個</rb><rt>bing-dzih-go</rt><rb>工鈿</rb><rt>*kong-din*</rt></ruby>。』<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>去了</rb><rt>kyʽi-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>毛</rb><rt>Mao</rt><rb>十二</rb><rt>jih-nyi</rt><rb>點鐘</rb><rt>tin-cong</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>下半日</rb><rt>ʽô-pun-nyih</rt><rb>三</rb><rt>sæn</rt><rb>點鐘</rb><rt>tin-cong</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>走出去</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>做法</rb><rt>tso-fah</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>毛</rb><rt>Mao</rt><rb>五</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>點鐘</rb><rt>tin-cong</rt><rb>走出去</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>空頭</rb><rt>kʽong-deo</rt><rb>立間</rb><rt>lih-kæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>咋會</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>淨日</rb><rt>zing-nyih</rt><rb>空頭</rb><rt>kʽong-deo</rt><rb>立東</rb><rt>lih-tong</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？』<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>嘸人</rb><rt>m-nying</rt><rb>僱</rb><rt>kwu</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt></ruby>。』<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>葡萄園裏</rb><rt>bu-dao-yün-li</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>平直個</rb><rt>bing-dzih-go</rt><rb>工鈿</rb><rt>*kong-din*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>馱個</rb><rt>do-go</rt></ruby>。』<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>Tao</rt><rb>夜快</rb><rt>yia-kwʽa</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>園主</rb><rt>yün-cü</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>把賬</rb><rt>pô-tsiang</rt><rb>先生</rb><rt>sin-sang</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>謳</rb><rt>Eo</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>工人</rb><rt>kong-nying</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>結煞個</rb><rt>kyih-sah-go</rt><rb>起頭</rb><rt>kyʽi-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一直</rb><rt>ih-dzih</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>頭一伐</rb><rt>deo-ih-vah</rt><rb>上工個</rb><rt>*zông-kong-go*</rt><rb>爲止</rb><rt>we-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>發</rb><rt>fah</rt><rb>工鈿</rb><rt>kong-din</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>。』<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>毛</rb><rt>Mao</rt><rb>五</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>點鐘</rb><rt>tin-cong</rt><rb>上工個</rb><rt>*zông-kong-go*</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>走來</rb><rt>tseo-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>各人</rb><rt>kôh-nying</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>馱</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>一鈿</rb><rt>ih-din</rt><rb>銀子</rb><rt>nying-ts</rt></ruby>。<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>頭一伐</rb><rt>Deo-ih-vah</rt><rb>上工個</rb><rt>*zông-kong-go*</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>道是</rb><rt>dao-z</rt><rb>其自</rb><rt>*gyi-zi*</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>馱勒</rb><rt>do-leh</rt><rb>多</rb><rt>to</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>各人</rb><rt>kôh-nying</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>馱勒</rb><rt>do-leh</rt><rb>一鈿</rb><rt>ih-din</rt><rb>銀子</rb><rt>nying-ts</rt></ruby>。<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>馱仔</rb><rt>do-ts</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>媢怨</rb><rt>mao-ün</rt><rb>家主</rb><rt>kô-cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：<sup>12</sup> 『<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>結煞</rb><rt>kyih-sah</rt><rb>上工個</rb><rt>*zông-kong-go*</rt><rb>只</rb><rt>*tsih*</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>一</rb><rt>ih</rt><rb>點鐘</rb><rt>tin-cong</rt><rb>工夫</rb><rt>kong-fu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>淨日</rb><rt>*zing*-nyih</rt><rb>火熱鐵塌</rb><rt>ho-nyih-tʽih-tʽah</rt><rb>勞碌</rb><rt>lao-loh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>倒</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>待</rb><rt>dæ</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>一樣</rb><rt>ih-yiang</rt><rb>嗎</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>！』<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>家主</rb><rt>Kô-cü</rt><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>朋友</rb><rt>Beng-yiu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>嘸告</rb><rt>m-kao</rt><rb>委曲</rb><rt>w̆e-kyʽüoh</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>講開</rb><rt>kông-kʽæ</rt><rb>啥</rb><rt>soh</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>一鈿</rb><rt>ih-din</rt><rb>銀子</rb><rt>nying-ts</rt><rb>嗎</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>爾自個</rb><rt>Ng-zi-go</rt><rb>工鈿</rb><rt>*kong-din*</rt><rb>馱勒</rb><rt>do-leh</rt><rb>走</rb><rt>tseo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>注心</rb><rt>cü-sing</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>結煞個</rb><rt>kyih-sah-go</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>一樣個</rb><rt>ih-yiang-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>自個</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>東西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>難道</rb><rt>næn-dao</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>主意</rb><rt>cü-i</rt><rb>嗎</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>爲了</rb><rt>We-leh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt><rb>妒忌心</rb><rt>tu-gyi-sing</rt><rb>嗎</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>Z-ka</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>在後個</rb><rt>dzæ-ʽeo-go</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>上前</rb><rt>zông-zin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>在前個</rb><rt>dzæ-zin-go</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>落後</rb><rt>lôh-ʽeo</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>召來個</rb><rt>dziao-læ-go</rt><rb>多</rb><rt>to</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>挑選個</rb><rt>tʽiao-shün-go</rt><rb>缺</rb><rt>kyʽüih</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>17</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>YIÆ-SU</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>上</rb><rt>zông</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶路撒冷</rb><rt>Yiæ-lu-sah-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>路上</rb><rt>lu-zông</rt><rb>帶勒</rb><rt>ta-leh</rt><rb>十二個</rb><rt>jih-nyi-go</rt><rb>門徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>私下</rb><rt>s-ʽô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：<sup>18</sup> 「<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>keh-tsao</rt><rb>上</rb><rt>zông</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶路撒冷</rb><rt>Yiæ-lu-sah-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>人個</rb><rt>Nying-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>Ng-ts</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>送</rb><rt>song</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>祭司頭</rb><rt>tsi-s-deo</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>讀書人</rb><rt>doh-shü-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>定</rb><rt>ding</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>死罪</rb><rt>si-ze</rt></ruby>，<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>解去</rb><rt>ka-kyʽi</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>別國人</rb><rt>bih-koh-nying</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>遭蹋</rb><rt>tsao-tʽah</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>鞭子</rb><rt>pin-ts</rt><rb>打</rb><rt>tang</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>十字架上</rb><rt>jih-z-kô-zông</rt><rb>釘煞</rb><rt>ting-sah</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>第三</rb><rt>di-sæn</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>活轉來</rb><rt>weh-cün-læ</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>西庇太</rb><rt>Si-pi-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>兒子個</rb><rt>ng-ts-go</rt><rb>阿孃</rb><rt>ah-nyiang</rt><rb>帶領</rb><rt>ta-ling</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>兩個</rb><rt>*liang-go*</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>走</rb><rt>tseo</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>身邊</rb><rt>sing-pin</rt><rb>拜</rb><rt>pa</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>求</rb><rt>gyiu</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>一樣</rb><rt>ih-yiang</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>。<sup>21</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>啥西</rb><rt>soh-si</rt></ruby>？」<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>Iao</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>兩</rb><rt>liang</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt><rb>坐</rb><rt>zo</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>Ng-go</rt><rb>國</rb><rt>koh</rt><rb>裏向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>順手邊</rb><rt>jing-siu-pin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>借手邊</rb><rt>tsia-siu-pin</rt></ruby>。」<sup>22</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>求個</rb><rt>gyiu-go</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>曉得個</rb><rt>hyiao-teh-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>將來</rb><rt>tsiang-læ</rt><rb>喫個</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-go</rt><rb>杯子</rb><rt>pe-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>能夠</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>喫</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>受個</rb><rt>ziu-go</rt><rb>洗禮</rb><rt>si-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>能夠</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>受</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt></ruby>？」<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>能夠個</rb><rt>Neng-keo-go</rt></ruby>。」<sup>23</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>杯子</rb><rt>pe-ts</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>喫個</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>受個</rb><rt>ziu-go</rt><rb>洗禮</rb><rt>si-li</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>受個</rb><rt>ziu-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt><rb>坐</rb><rt>zo</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>順手邊</rb><rt>jing-siu-pin</rt><rb>借手邊</rb><rt>tsia-siu-pin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>單只</rb><rt>tæn-tsih</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>Ah-tia</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>預備好間個</rb><rt>yü-be-hao-kæn-go</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>24</sup> <ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>十個</rb><rt>jih-go</rt><rb>門徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>一</rb><rt>ih</rt><rb>聽聞</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>兩</rb><rt>liang</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>怏了</rb><rt>ông-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>25</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>謳</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>別國人個</rb><rt>bih-koh-nying-go</rt><rb>皇帝</rb><rt>wông-ti</rt><rb>壓實</rb><rt>ah-jih</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>大個</rb><rt>do-go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>執掌</rb><rt>tsih-công</rt><rb>權柄</rb><rt>gyün-ping</rt><rb>管住</rb><rt>kwun-djü</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>曉得個</rb><rt>hyiao-teh-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>26</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>嘸得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>是介個</rb><rt>z-ka-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>想</rb><rt>siang</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>大個</rb><rt>do-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>該</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>傭人</rb><rt>yüong-nying</rt></ruby>；<sup>27</sup> <ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>想</rb><rt>siang</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>頭個</rb><rt>deo-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>該</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>奴僕</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt></ruby>。<sup>28</sup> <ruby><rb>正像</rb><rt>tsing-ziang</rt><rb>人個</rb><rt>Nying-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>Ng-ts</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt><rb>服侍</rb><rt>voh-z</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>服侍</rb><rt>voh-z</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>捨出</rb><rt>sô-cʽih</rt><rb>自個</rb><rt>Zi-go</rt><rb>性命</rb><rt>sing-ming</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>許多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>人個</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>替代</rb><rt>tʽi-dæ</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>29</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>GYI-LAH</rt><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶利哥</rb><rt>Yiæ-li-ko</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一大潮</rb><rt>ih-do-dziao</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>跟着</rb><rt>keng-djôh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>扣扣</rb><rt>Kʽeo-kʽeo</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>兩個</rb><rt>liang-go</rt><rb>瞎子</rb><rt>hah-ts</rt><rb>路</rb><rt>lu</rt><rb>邊沿</rb><rt>pin-yin</rt><rb>坐間</rb><rt>zo-kæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>聽聞</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>走過</rb><rt>tseo-ko</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>響響</rb><rt>hyiang-hyiang</rt><rb>謳起來</rb><rt>eo-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb><u>大闢</u>個</rb><rt>Da-bih-go</rt><rb>後代</rb><rt>ʽEo-dæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>可憐</rb><rt>kʽo-lin</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt></ruby>。」<sup>31</sup> <ruby><rb>箇潮</rb><rt>Keh-dziao</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>狠</rb><rt>heng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>謳</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>嘸得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>響</rb><rt>hyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>反轉</rb><rt>fæn-cün</rt><rb>愈加</rb><rt>yü-kô</rt><rb>謳勒</rb><rt>eo-leh</rt><rb>響</rb><rt>hyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb><u>大闢</u>個</rb><rt>Da-bih-go</rt><rb>後代</rb><rt>ʽEo-dæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>可憐</rb><rt>kʽo-lin</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt></ruby>。」<sup>32</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>立落</rb><rt>lih-lôh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>謳</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>Iao</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>啥西</rb><rt>soh-si</rt></ruby>？」<sup>33</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>*iao*</rt><rb>開</rb><rt>kʽæ</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>眼睛</rb><rt>ngæn-tsing</rt></ruby>。」<sup>34</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>發</rb><rt>fah</rt><rb>慈悲心</rb><rt>dz-pe-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>按按</rb><rt>en-en</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>眼睛</rb><rt>ngæn-tsing</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>眼睛</rb><rt>ngæn-tsing</rt><rb>立刻</rb><rt>lih-kʽeh</rt><rb>看見了</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>跟勒</rb><rt>keng-leh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>去了</rb><rt>kyʽi-de</rt></ruby>。

---

### Mt. XXI.

<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>GYI-LAH</rt><rb>近攏</rb><rt>gying-long</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶路撒冷</rb><rt>Yiæ-lu-sah-leng</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>伯法基</rb><rt>Pah-fah-kyi</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>橄欖</rb><rt>Ken-læn</rt><rb>山</rb><rt>sæn</rt><rb>邊沿</rb><rt>pin-yin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>keh-tsao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>兩個</rb><rt>liang-go</rt><rb>門徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：<sup>2</sup> 「<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>對頭個</rb><rt>te-deo-go</rt><rb>鄉村</rb><rt>hyiang-tsʽeng</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>碰着</rb><rt>pʽong-djôh</rt><rb>一匹</rb><rt>ih-pʽih</rt><rb>雌驢</rb><rt>tsʽ-li</rt><rb>樁間</rb><rt>công-kæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>一匹</rb><rt>ih-pʽih</rt><rb>小驢</rb><rt>siao-li</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>來間</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>解仔</rb><rt>ka-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>牽來</rb><rt>kyʽin-læ</rt></ruby>。<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>Ziah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>Z</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>用着</rb><rt>yüong-djôh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>。』<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。」<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>一切個</rb><rt>ih-tsʽih-go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>做法</rb><rt>tso-fah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>先知人</rb><rt>sin-cü-nying</rt><rb>講過了個</rb><rt>kông-ko-liao-go</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>效驗</rb><rt>yiao-nyiæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：<sup>5</sup> 「<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>Hao</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>郇</u>個</rb><rt>Shing-go</rt><rb>囡</rb><rt>nön</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>爾個</rb><rt>Ng-go</rt><rb>皇帝</rb><rt>Wông-ti</rt><rb>低聲小意</rb><rt>ti-sing-siao-i</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>騎勒</rb><rt>gyi-leh</rt><rb>一匹</rb><rt>ih-pʽih</rt><rb>驢子</rb><rt>li-ts</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>一匹</rb><rt>ih-pʽih</rt><rb>小驢</rb><rt>siao-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>烏碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>來了</rb><rt>læ-de</rt></ruby>。』」 

<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>門徒</rb><rt>Meng-du</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>依</rb><rt>i</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>，<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>牽勒</rb><rt>kyʽin-leh</rt><rb>雌驢</rb><rt>tsʽ-li</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>小驢</rb><rt>siao-li</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>自個</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>衣裳</rb><rt>i-zông</rt><rb>放</rb><rt>fông</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>上頭</rb><rt>zông-deo</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>騎上去</rb><rt>gyi-zông-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>一大潮</rb><rt>ih-do-dziao</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>馱勒</rb><rt>do-leh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>衣裳</rb><rt>i-zông</rt><rb>鋪</rb><rt>pʽu</rt><rb>路</rb><rt>lu</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>還有星</rb><rt>wa-yiu-sing</rt><rb>斫勒</rb><rt>tsôh-leh</rt><rb>樹</rb><rt>jü</rt><rb>椏枝</rb><rt>ô-ts</rt><rb>鋪</rb><rt>pʽu</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>路裏</rb><rt>lu-li</rt></ruby>。<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>前後</rb><rt>Zin-ʽeo</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>兩潮</rb><rt>liang-dziao</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>響響</rb><rt>hyiang-hyiang</rt><rb>謳起來</rb><rt>eo-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>和散𠺶</rb><rt>ʽO-sæn-nah</rt></ruby><sup> </sup><ruby><rb>歸</rb><rt>kwe</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>大闢</u>個</rb><rt>Da-bih-go</rt><rb>後代</rb><rt>ʽEo-dæ</rt></ruby>！<ruby><rb>靠着</rb><rt>Kʽao-djôh</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>名頭</rb><rt>ming-deo</rt><rb>來個</rb><rt>læ-go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>Cü-kwu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>應該</rb><rt>ing-kæ</rt><rb>祝讚個</rb><rt>coh-tsæn-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>Læ</rt><rb>頂</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>高</rb><rt>kao</rt><rb>烏蕩</rb><rt>*u-dông*</rt><rb>和散𠺶</rb><rt>ʽO-sæn-nah</rt></ruby>！」

<sup>\[1\] <u>希伯來</u>語 Hosanna，讚美神的用語</sup>

<sup>10</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>一</rb><rt>ih</rt><rb>走進</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶路撒冷</rb><rt>Yiæ-lu-sah-leng</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>合城裏</rb><rt>ʽeh-zing-li</rt><rb>人聲</rb><rt>*nying-sing*</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>搖動</rb><rt>yiao-dong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>啥個</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>？」<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>箇潮</rb><rt>Keh-dziao</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>加利利</rb><rt>Kyüô-li-li</rt></ruby></u> <ruby><rb><u>拿撒勒</u>個</rb><rt>Nô-sah-leh-go</rt><rb>先知</rb><rt>Sin-cü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u>。」

<sup>12</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>走進</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>聖殿裏</rb><rt>sing-din-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>趕出</rb><rt>ken-cʽih</rt><rb>聖殿裏</rb><rt>sing-din-li</rt><rb>攏總</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>買賣</rb><rt>ma-ma</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>推倒</rb><rt>tʽe-tao</rt><rb>兌</rb><rt>de</rt><rb>銅鈿</rb><rt>dong-din</rt><rb>主顧個</rb><rt>cü-kwu-go</rt><rb>櫃頭</rb><rt>gyü-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>賣</rb><rt>ma</rt><rb>鵓鴿</rb><rt>beh-keh</rt><rb>主顧個</rb><rt>cü-kwu-go</rt><rb>椅子</rb><rt>ü-ts</rt></ruby>，<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>書裏</rb><rt>*Shü-li*</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>寫的</rb><rt>sia-tih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>我個</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>屋落</rb><rt>oh-lô</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>稱呼</rb><rt>tsʽing-hwu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>禱告個</rb><rt>tao-kao-go</rt><rb>屋落</rb><rt>oh-lô</rt></ruby>。』 <ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>倒</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>賊窠</rb><rt>zeh-kʽo</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>。」<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>Læ</rt><rb>聖殿裏</rb><rt>sing-din-li</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>瞎子</rb><rt>hah-ts</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>拐腳</rb><rt>kwa-kyiah</rt><rb>走攏</rb><rt>tseo-long</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>耶穌</u>個</rb><rt>Yiæ-su-go</rt><rb>身邊</rb><rt>sing-pin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>醫</rb><rt>i</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt></ruby>。<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>祭司頭</rb><rt>tsi-s-deo</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>讀書人</rb><rt>doh-shü-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>做出來個</rb><rt>tso-cʽih-læ-go</rt><rb>稀奇</rb><rt>hyi-gyi</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>小人</rb><rt>siao-nying</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>聖殿裏</rb><rt>sing-din-li</rt><rb>懷懷響</rb><rt>wæ-wæ-hyiang</rt><rb>來間</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>和散𠺶</rb><rt>ʽO-sæn-nah</rt><rb>歸</rb><rt>kwe</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>大闢</u>個</rb><rt>Da-bih-go</rt><rb>後代</rb><rt>ʽEo-dæ</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>怏了</rb><rt>ông-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：<sup>16</sup> 「<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>話個</rb><rt>wô-go</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>聽聞</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt></ruby>？」<u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>聽聞個</rb><rt>Tʽing-meng-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>書裏</rb><rt>*Shü-li*</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>*sô*</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>*wô*</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>從</rb><rt>Dzong</rt><rb>小人</rb><rt>siao-nying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>奶花個</rb><rt>na-hwun-go</rt><rb>口裏</rb><rt>kʽeo-li</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>顯出</rb><rt>hyin-cʽih</rt><rb>讚美</rb><rt>tsæn-me</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，』 <ruby><rb>箇句</rb><rt>*keh-kyü*</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>*shih-wô*</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>啥</rb><rt>soh</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>讀過</rb><rt>doh-ko</rt><rb>嗎</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？」<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>Ziu</rt><rb>離開</rb><rt>li-kʽæ</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt><rb>城外</rb><rt>zing-nga</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>伯大尼</rb><rt>Pah-da-nyi</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>箇頭</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>宿過</rb><rt>soh-ko</rt><rb>夜</rb><rt>yia</rt></ruby>。

<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>早</rb><rt>TSAO</rt><rb>天亮</rb><rt>tʽin-nyiang</rt><rb>轉來</rb><rt>cün-læ</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>城裏</rb><rt>zing-li</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>肚飢了</rb><rt>du-kyi-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>路</rb><rt>Lu</rt><rb>邊沿</rb><rt>pin-yin</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>一株</rb><rt>ih-cü</rt><rb>無花果</rb><rt>vu-hwô-ko</rt><rb>樹</rb><rt>jü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>走攏去</rb><rt>tseo-long-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>只</rb><rt>tsih</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>葉</rb><rt>yih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m̆-teh</rt><rb>別樣告</rb><rt>bih-nyiang-kao</rt><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>朝勒</rb><rt>dziao-leh</rt><rb>箇株</rb><rt>keh-cü</rt><rb>樹</rb><rt>jü</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>以後</rb><rt>yi-ʽeo</rt><rb>永</rb><rt>üong</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>結</rb><rt>kyih</rt><rb>果子</rb><rt>ko-ts</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>無花果</rb><rt>Vu-hwô-ko</rt><rb>樹</rb><rt>jü</rt><rb>立刻</rb><rt>lih-kʽeh</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>枯了</rb><rt>kwʽu-de</rt></ruby>。

<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>門徒</rb><rt>Meng-du</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>稀奇</rb><rt>hyi-gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>無花果</rb><rt>Vu-hwô-ko</rt><rb>樹</rb><rt>jü</rt><rb>枯勒</rb><rt>kwʽu-leh</rt><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>快</rb><rt>kwʽa</rt></ruby>！」<sup>21</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>真</rb><rt>tsing</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>相信個</rb><rt>siang-sing-go</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一眼</rb><rt>ih-ngæn</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>疑惑</rb><rt>nyi-ʽôh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>非但</rb><rt>fi-dæn</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>無花果</rb><rt>vu-hwô-ko</rt><rb>樹個</rb><rt>jü-go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>朝勒</rb><rt>dziao-leh</rt><rb>箇座</rb><rt>keh-zo</rt><rb>山</rb><rt>sæn</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>Hao</rt><rb>移過去</rb><rt>yi-ko-kyʽi</rt><rb>投落</rb><rt>deo-lôh</rt><rb>海裏</rb><rt>hæ-li</rt></ruby>，』<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>做成</rb><rt>tso-dzing</rt></ruby>。<sup>22</sup> <ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>Wa-yiu</rt><rb>禱告</rb><rt>tao-kao</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>求個</rb><rt>gyiu-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>只</rb><rt>*tsih*</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>*iao*</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>23</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>YIÆ-SU</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>走進</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>聖殿裏</rb><rt>sing-din-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>扣扣</rb><rt>kʽeo-kʽeo</rt><rb>來間</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇班</rb><rt>keh-pæn</rt><rb>祭司頭</rb><rt>tsi-s-deo</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>百姓個</rb><rt>pah-sing-go</rt><rb>長老</rb><rt>tsiang-lao</rt><rb>走攏來</rb><rt>tseo-long-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>啥個</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>權柄</rb><rt>gyün-ping</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>誰</rb><rt>jü</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>權柄</rb><rt>gyün-ping</rt></ruby>？」<sup>24</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>問</rb><rt>meng</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>一句</rb><rt>ih-kyü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>一句</rb><rt>ih-kyü</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>啥個</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>權柄</rb><rt>gyün-ping</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>。<sup>25</sup> <ruby><rb><u>約翰</u>個</rb><rt>Iah-ʽen-go</rt><rb>洗禮</rb><rt>si-li</rt><rb>何裏</rb><rt>ʽah-li</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>還是</rb><rt>wa-z</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>天</rb><rt>tʽin</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>*læ*</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>還是</rb><rt>wa-z</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>*læ*</rt></ruby>？」<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>私下</rb><rt>s-ʽô</rt><rb>商量</rb><rt>siang-liang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>Ziah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>Z</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>天</rb><rt>tʽin</rt><rb>來個</rb><rt>*læ-go*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>Ka-ni</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>咋會</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>？』<sup>26</sup> <ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>Ziah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>Z</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>來個</rb><rt>*læ-go*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>怕</rb><rt>pʽô</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>算</rb><rt>sön</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>約翰</rb><rt>Iah-ʽen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>是個</rb><rt>z-go</rt><rb>先知人</rb><rt>sin-cü-nying</rt></ruby>。」<sup>27</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>Ziu</rt><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt></ruby>。」<u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>啥個</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>權柄</rb><rt>gyün-ping</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>。

<sup>28</sup> 「<ruby><rb>爾拉個</rb><rt>Ng-lah-go</rt><rb>意思</rb><rt>i-s</rt><rb>咋個</rb><rt>dza-go</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>兩個</rb><rt>liang-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>走來</rb><rt>tseo-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>大個</rb><rt>do-go</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>兒子</rb><rt>Ng-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>今末</rb><rt>kyih-mih</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>葡萄園裏</rb><rt>bu-dao-yün-li</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>生活</rb><rt>sang-weh</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。』<sup>29</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>𣍐</rb><rt>ve</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>*kyʽi*</rt></ruby>。』<ruby><rb>後頭</rb><rt>ʽeo-deo</rt><rb>懊悔</rb><rt>ao-hwe</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>去了</rb><rt>kyʽi-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>30</sup> <ruby><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>*Ah-tia*</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>走來</rb><rt>tseo-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>第二個</rb><rt>di-nyi-go</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>講法</rb><rt>kông-fah</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>去了</rb><rt>*kyʽi-de*</rt></ruby>。』<ruby><rb>只是</rb><rt>tsih-z</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>31</sup> <ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>兩個</rb><rt>liang-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>何裏</rb><rt>ʽah-li</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>依</rb><rt>i</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>ah-tia</rt><rb>心想</rb><rt>sing-siang</rt><rb>做個</rb><rt>tso-go</rt></ruby>？」<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>大個</rb><rt>do-go</rt></ruby>。」<u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>真</rb><rt>tsing</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>收錢糧</rb><rt>Siu-zin-liang</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>婊子</rb><rt>piao-ts</rt><rb>還</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>先如</rb><rt>sin-jü</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>走進</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>神國裏</rb><rt>Jing-koh-li</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>32</sup> <ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>約翰</rb><rt>Iah-ʽen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>正直個</rb><rt>tsing-dzih-go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>烏碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>收錢糧</rb><rt>siu-zin-liang</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>婊子</rb><rt>piao-ts</rt><rb>反轉</rb><rt>fæn-cün</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>之後</rb><rt>ts-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>懊悔</rb><rt>ao-hwe</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>33</sup> 「<ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>Wa-yiu</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>比方</rb><rt>pi-fông</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>聽</rb><rt>tʽing</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>家主</rb><rt>kô-cü</rt><rb>種</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>一園</rb><rt>ih-yün</rt><rb>紫葡萄</rb><rt>ts-bu-dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>團圈</rb><rt>dön-kyʽün</rt><rb>打勒</rb><rt>tang-leh</rt><rb>槍笆</rb><rt>tsʽiang-pô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>園裏</rb><rt>yün-li</rt><rb>掘</rb><rt>gyüih</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>酒榨窞</rb><rt>tsiu-tsô-den</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>造</rb><rt>zao</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>高樓</rb><rt>kao-leo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>租出</rb><rt>tsu-cʽih</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>種田人</rb><rt>cong-din-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>出門</rb><rt>cʽih-meng</rt><rb>去了</rb><rt>kyʽi-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>34</sup> <ruby><rb>果子</rb><rt>Ko-ts</rt><rb>熟個</rb><rt>*joh-go*</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>快</rb><rt>kwʽa</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>奴僕</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>種田人</rb><rt>cong-din-nying</rt><rb>烏碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>收</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>園裏個</rb><rt>yün-li-go</rt><rb>果子</rb><rt>ko-ts</rt></ruby>。<sup>35</sup> <ruby><rb>種田人</rb><rt>Cong-din-nying</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>抲</rb><rt>kʽô</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>奴僕</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>打</rb><rt>tang</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>殺</rb><rt>sah</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>石頭</rb><rt>zah-deo</rt><rb>揞煞</rb><rt>kʽang-sah</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>36</sup> <ruby><rb>家主</rb><rt>*Kô-cü*</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>別個</rb><rt>bih-go</rt><rb>奴僕</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>比</rb><rt>pi</rt><rb>前頭</rb><rt>zin-deo</rt><rb>還</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>多</rb><rt>to</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>種田人</rb><rt>*cong-din-nying*</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>弄</rb><rt>long</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>。<sup>37</sup> <ruby><rb>後頭</rb><rt>ʽEo-deo</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>自個</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>敬重</rb><rt>kying-djong</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby>。』<sup>38</sup> <ruby><rb>種田人</rb><rt>Cong-din-nying</rt><rb>一</rb><rt>ih</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>*gyi*</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>接着</rb><rt>tsih-djôh</rt><rb>產業</rb><rt>tsʽæn-nyih</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>且</rb><rt>tsʽia</rt><rb>殺勒</rb><rt>sah-leh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>奪</rb><rt>deh</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>產業</rb><rt>tsʽæn-nyih</rt></ruby>。』<sup>39</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>Ziu</rt><rb>抲勒</rb><rt>kʽô-leh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>推出</rb><rt>tʽe-cʽih</rt><rb>園外</rb><rt>yün-nga</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>殺掉</rb><rt>sah-diao</rt></ruby>。<sup>40</sup> <ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>Ka-ni</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>居園個</rb><rt>ke-yün-go</rt><rb>主人家</rb><rt>cü-nying-kô</rt><rb>歸來</rb><rt>kyü-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>辦</rb><rt>bæn</rt><rb>箇班</rb><rt>keh-pæn</rt><rb>種田人</rb><rt>cong-din-nying</rt></ruby>？」<sup>41</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>會</rb><rt>We</rt><rb>煞扣</rb><rt>sah-kʽeo</rt><rb>抄滅</rb><rt>tsʽao-mih</rt><rb>箇班</rb><rt>keh-pæn</rt><rb>惡人</rb><rt>ôh-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>葡萄園</rb><rt>bu-dao-yün</rt><rb>租出</rb><rt>tsu-cʽih</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>別個</rb><rt>bih-go</rt><rb>種田人</rb><rt>cong-din-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>果子</rb><rt>ko-ts</rt><rb>熟個</rb><rt>*joh-go*</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>馱出</rb><rt>do-cʽih</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>42</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>聖書裏</rb><rt>Sing-shü-li</rt><rb>寫的</rb><rt>*sia-tih*</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>起屋個</rb><rt>Kyʽi-oh-go</rt><rb>師父</rb><rt>s-vu</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>棄掉</rb><rt>kyʽi-diao</rt><rb>箇塊</rb><rt>keh-kwʽe</rt><rb>石頭</rb><rt>zah-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>做勒</rb><rt>tso-leh</rt><rb>屋</rb><rt>*oh*</rt><rb>角落頭</rb><rt>kôh-lôh-den</rt><rb>頭一塊</rb><rt>deo-ih-kwʽe</rt><rb>石頭</rb><rt>zah-deo</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>做個</rb><rt>tso-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>dziu</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>看來</rb><rt>kʽen-læ</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>稀奇個</rb><rt>hyi-gyi-go</rt></ruby>。』 <ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>*keh-go*</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>*shih-wô*</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>啥</rb><rt>soh</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>讀過</rb><rt>doh-ko</rt><rb>嗎</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<sup>43</sup> <ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>Keh-lah</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>國</rb><rt>koh</rt><rb>必</rb><rt>pih</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>爾拉個</rb><rt>ng-lah-go</rt><rb>手裏</rb><rt>siu-li</rt><rb>奪勒去</rb><rt>deh-leh-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>賜撥</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>一班</rb><rt>ih-pæn</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>結</rb><rt>kyih</rt><rb>神國個</rb><rt>Jing-koh-go</rt><rb>果子</rb><rt>ko-ts</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt></ruby>。<sup>44</sup> <ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>Wa-yiu</rt><rb>跌落</rb><rt>tih-lôh</rt><rb>箇塊</rb><rt>keh-kwʽe</rt><rb>石頭上</rb><rt>zah-deo-zông</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>跌糊</rb><rt>tih-wu</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇塊</rb><rt>*keh-kwʽe*</rt><rb>石頭</rb><rt>*zah-deo*</rt><rb>跌落</rb><rt>tih-lôh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>上頭</rb><rt>zông-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>壓勒</rb><rt>ah-leh</rt><rb>粉碎</rb><rt>feng-se</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>45</sup> <ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>祭司頭</rb><rt>tsi-s-deo</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法利賽</rb><rt>Fah-li-sæ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>聽聞</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>比方</rb><rt>pi-fông</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>講到</rb><rt>kông-tao</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt></ruby>。<sup>46</sup> <ruby><rb>心想</rb><rt>Sing-siang</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>抲</rb><rt>kʽô</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>只是</rb><rt>*tsih-z*</rt><rb>怕</rb><rt>pʽô</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>算</rb><rt>sön</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>是個</rb><rt>z-go</rt><rb>先知人</rb><rt>sin-cü-nying</rt></ruby>。

### Mt. XXII.

<u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>開</rb><rt>kʽæ</rt><rb>口</rb><rt>kʽeo</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>比方</rb><rt>pi-fông</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：——

<sup>2</sup> 「<ruby><rb>天國</rb><rt>Tʽin-koh</rt><rb>可比</rb><rt>kʽo-pi</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>皇帝</rb><rt>wông-ti</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>辦</rb><rt>bæn</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>兒子個</rb><rt>ng-ts-go</rt><rb>好日酒</rb><rt>hao-nyih-tsiu</rt></ruby>。<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>奴僕</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>催</rb><rt>tsʽe</rt><rb>請間個</rb><rt>tsʽing-kæn-go</rt><rb>人客</rb><rt>nying-kʽah</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>喫</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt><rb>好日酒</rb><rt>hao-nyih-tsiu</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>肯</rb><rt>kʽeng</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>Yi</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>別個</rb><rt>bih-go</rt><rb>奴僕</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>Hao</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>請間個</rb><rt>tsʽing-kæn-go</rt><rb>人客</rb><rt>nying-kʽah</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我個</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>酒飯</rb><rt>tsiu-væn</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>備好了</rb><rt>be-hao-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>牛</rb><rt>ngeo</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>壯壯</rb><rt>công-công</rt><rb>牲口</rb><rt>sang-kʽeo</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>殺好</rb><rt>sah-hao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>樣樣</rb><rt>yiang-yiang</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>齊備了</rb><rt>zi-be-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好日酒</rb><rt>hao-nyih-tsiu</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>喫了</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-de</rt></ruby>。』<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>睬</rb><rt>tsʽæ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>走了去了</rb><rt>tseo-leh-kyʽi-de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>田頭</rb><rt>din-deo</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>*kyʽi*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>生意</rb><rt>sang-i</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>*kyʽi*</rt></ruby>。<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>皇帝</rb><rt>Wông-ti</rt><rb>一</rb><rt>ih</rt><rb>聽聞</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>發怏了</rb><rt>fah-ông-de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>兵馬</rb><rt>ping-mô</rt><rb>除滅</rb><rt>djü-mih</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>兇手</rb><rt>hyüong-siu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>燒掉</rb><rt>siao-diao</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>城裏</rb><rt>zing-li</rt></ruby>。<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>Keh-tsao</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>奴僕</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>好日酒</rb><rt>Hao-nyih-tsiu</rt><rb>備好東</rb><rt>be-hao-tong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>只是</rb><rt>tsih-z</rt><rb>請間個</rb><rt>tsʽing-kæn-go</rt><rb>人客</rb><rt>nying-kʽah</rt><rb>欠</rb><rt>kyʽin</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt></ruby>。<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>Ka-ni</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>四岔路口</rb><rt>s-tsʽô-lu-kʽeo</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>碰着個</rb><rt>pʽong-djôh-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>謳</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>喫</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt><rb>好日酒</rb><rt>hao-nyih-tsiu</rt></ruby>。』<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>奴僕</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt><rb>路裏</rb><rt>lu-li</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>碰着個</rb><rt>pʽong-djôh-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好好歹歹</rb><rt>hao-hao-tæ-tæ</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>領</rb><rt>ling</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>進來</rb><rt>tsing-læ</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>喫</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt><rb>好日酒</rb><rt>hao-nyih-tsiu</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>人客</rb><rt>nying-kʽah</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>坐滿了</rb><rt>zo-mun-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>皇帝</rb><rt>Wông-ti</rt><rb>走進來</rb><rt>tseo-tsing-læ</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt><rb>坐席個</rb><rt>zo-zih-go</rt><rb>人客</rb><rt>nying-kʽah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>穿</rb><rt>cʽün</rt><rb>賀</rb><rt>ʽo</rt><rb>好日</rb><rt>hao-nyih</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>大衣</rb><rt>*do*-i</rt></ruby>，<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>朋友</rb><rt>Beng-yiu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>穿</rb><rt>cʽün</rt><rb>賀</rb><rt>ʽo</rt><rb>好日</rb><rt>hao-nyih</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>大衣</rb><rt>do-i</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>咋會</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>走進</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>蕩頭</rb><rt>dông-deo</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>？』<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>開</rb><rt>kʽæ</rt><rb>口</rb><rt>kʽeo</rt></ruby>。<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>Keh-tsao</rt><rb>皇帝</rb><rt>wông-ti</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>底下人</rb><rt>ti-ʽô-nying</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>Hao</rt><rb>縛</rb><rt>bo</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>腳</rb><rt>kyiah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>拖出</rb><rt>tʽo-cʽih</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>外頭</rb><rt>nga-deo</rt><rb>黑暗個</rb><rt>heh-en-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇頭</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>咬勒</rb><rt>ngao-leh</rt><rb>牙齒</rb><rt>ngô-tsʽ</rt><rb>啼哭個</rb><rt>di-kʽoh-go</rt><rb>聲響</rb><rt>sing-hyiang</rt></ruby>。』<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>召來個</rb><rt>dziao-læ-go</rt><rb>多</rb><rt>to</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>挑選個</rb><rt>tʽiao-shün-go</rt><rb>缺</rb><rt>kyʽüih</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>KEH-GO</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法利賽</rb><rt>Fah-li-sæ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>商量</rb><rt>siang-liang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>咋個</rb><rt>dza-go</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>將</rb><rt>tsiang</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>*Gyi-go*</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>裝</rb><rt>tsông</rt><rb>箍套圈</rb><rt>kwʽu-tʽao-kyʽün</rt><rb>套</rb><rt>tʽao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>Ziu</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>自個</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>門徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>希律</rb><rt>Hyi-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>箇黨</rb><rt>keh-tông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>問</rb><rt>meng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>先生</rb><rt>Sin-sang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>誠實個</rb><rt>dzing-jih-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>實實惠惠</rb><rt>jih-jih-we-we</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一眼</rb><rt>ih-ngæn</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>懼脅</rb><rt>gyü-hyih</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>准</rb><rt>cing</rt><rb>人家個</rb><rt>nying-kô-go</rt><rb>面孔</rb><rt>min-kʽong</rt></ruby>。<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>Ka-ni</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾個</rb><rt>Ng-go</rt><rb>意思</rb><rt>i-s</rt><rb>咋個</rb><rt>dza-go</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>頭</rb><rt>deo</rt><rb>錢糧</rb><rt>zin-liang</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>該撒</rb><rt>Kæ-sah</rt></ruby></u><sup> </sup>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt></ruby>？」<sup>18</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>其拉個</rb><rt>gyi-lah-go</rt><rb>惡意</rb><rt>ôh-i</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>假好人</rb><rt>Kô-hao-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>咋會</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>想</rb><rt>siang</rt><rb>試試</rb><rt>s-s</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>？<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>投</rb><rt>Deo</rt><rb>錢糧</rb><rt>zin-liang</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>通行錢</rb><rt>tʽong-ʽang-dzin</rt><rb>馱來</rb><rt>do-læ</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>馱</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>十塊</rb><rt>jih-kwʽe</rt><rb>番餅</rb><rt>fæn-ping</rt></ruby><sup> </sup><ruby><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>20</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>頭子</rb><rt>deo-ts</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>國號</rb><rt>koh-ʽao</rt></ruby><sup> </sup><ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>誰儂個</rb><rt>jü-noh-go</rt></ruby>？」<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>Z</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>該撒</u>個</rb><rt>Kæ-sah-go</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>Keh-tsao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>Ka-ni</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>該撒</u>個</rb><rt>Kæ-sah-go</rt><rb>東西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>該撒</rb><rt>Kæ-sah</rt></ruby></u>；<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>東西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>*peh*</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>。」<sup>22</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>一</rb><rt>ih</rt><rb>聽聞</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>稀奇</rb><rt>hyi-gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>離開</rb><rt>li-kʽæ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>去了</rb><rt>kyʽi-de</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] Kæ-sah *ziu-z* *Lo-mô* *wông-ti-go* *koh-ʽao*. <u>該撒</u>就是<u>羅馬</u>皇帝個國號。（原註）</sup>

<sup>\[2\] *Keh-kwʽe* *fæn-ping* *dzih* *ih-din* *nying-ts* *go*: *kʽen*. Mt. 20. 該塊番餅值一鈿銀子個。看 Mt. 20. 2（原註）</sup>

<sup>\[3\] *Peng-veng* *wô* z-ngæn. 本文話字眼。（原註）</sup>

<sup>23</sup> <u><ruby><rb>撒土該</rb><rt>Sah-tʽu-kæ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>活轉來個</rb><rt>weh-cün-læ-go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>嘸得個</rb><rt>m̆-teh-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>日子</rb><rt>nyih-ts</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>有星</rb><rt>yiu-sing</rt><rb>走攏來</rb><rt>tseo-long-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>問</rb><rt>meng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：<sup>24</sup> 「<ruby><rb>先生</rb><rt>Sin-sang</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>兒囡</rb><rt>ng-nô</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>死了</rb><rt>si-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿弟</rb><rt>ah-di</rt><rb>該</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>娶</rb><rt>cʽü</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>老𡢿</rb><rt>lao-nyüing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>阿哥</rb><rt>ah-ko</rt><rb>傳宗接代</rb><rt>djün-cong-tsih-dæ</rt></ruby>。<sup>25</sup> <ruby><rb>難間</rb><rt>Næn-kæn</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>烏碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>七</rb><rt>tsʽih</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>大個</rb><rt>do-go</rt><rb>抬了</rb><rt>dæ-leh</rt><rb>老𡢿</rb><rt>lao-nyüing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>死了</rb><rt>si-de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>兒囡</rb><rt>ng-nô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>老𡢿</rb><rt>lao-nyüing</rt><rb>遺落</rb><rt>yi-lôh</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>阿弟</rb><rt>ah-di</rt></ruby>。<sup>26</sup> <ruby><rb>第二</rb><rt>di-nyi</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>第三</rb><rt>di-sæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一直</rb><rt>ih-dzih</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>第七</rb><rt>di-tsʽih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>是介個</rb><rt>z-ka-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>27</sup> <ruby><rb>頂</rb><rt>Ting</rt><rb>結煞</rb><rt>kyih-sah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>老𡢿</rb><rt>lao-nyüing</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>死了</rb><rt>si-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>28</sup> <ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>Ka-ni</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>活轉來個</rb><rt>weh-cün-læ-go</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>七個</rb><rt>tsʽih-go</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>到底</rb><rt>tao-ti</rt><rb>算</rb><rt>sön</rt><rb>誰個</rb><rt>jü-go</rt><rb>老𡢿</rb><rt>lao-nyüing</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>娶</rb><rt>cʽü</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>過了</rb><rt>ko-de</rt></ruby>。」<sup>29</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>聖書</rb><rt>Sing-shü</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>能幹</rb><rt>neng-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>keh-lah</rt><rb>錯了</rb><rt>tsʽo-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>30</sup> <ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>活轉來個</rb><rt>weh-cün-læ-go</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>作</rb><rt>tsôh</rt><rb>娶進</rb><rt>cʽü-tsing</rt><rb>嫁出</rb><rt>kô-cʽih</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>天使</rb><rt>tʽin-s</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>天上</rb><rt>tʽin-zông</rt><rb>一樣個</rb><rt>ih-yiang-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>31</sup> <ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>Ziah</rt><rb>講到</rb><rt>kông-tao</rt><rb>死人</rb><rt>si-nying</rt><rb>活轉來</rb><rt>weh-cün-læ</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<sup>32</sup> 『<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>亞伯拉罕</u>個</rb><rt>Üô-pah-lah-hen-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb><u>以撒</u>個</rb><rt>Yi-sah-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb><u>雅各</u>個</rb><rt>Yüô-kôh-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>，』 <ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>啥</rb><rt>soh</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>讀過</rb><rt>doh-ko</rt><rb>嗎</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>死人個</rb><rt>si-nying-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>活人個</rb><rt>weh-nying-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>*Jing-ming*</rt></ruby>。」<sup>33</sup> <ruby><rb>百姓</rb><rt>Pah-sing</rt><rb>聽聞</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>稀奇</rb><rt>hyi-gyi</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>教訓</rb><rt>kao-hyüing</rt></ruby>。

<sup>34</sup> <u><ruby><rb>法利賽</rb><rt>Fah-li-sæ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>聽聞</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>講勒</rb><rt>kông-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>撒土該</rb><rt>Sah-tʽu-kæ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>𣍐</rb><rt>ve</rt><rb>開</rb><rt>kʽæ</rt><rb>口</rb><rt>kʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>聚集攏</rb><rt>jü-jih-long</rt></ruby>。<sup>35</sup> <ruby><rb>內中</rb><rt>Nen-cong</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>教</rb><rt>kao</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>先生</rb><rt>sin-sang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>試試</rb><rt>s-s</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>問</rb><rt>meng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：<sup>36</sup> 「<ruby><rb>先生</rb><rt>Sin-sang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt><rb>裏頭</rb><rt>li-deo</rt><rb>何裏</rb><rt>ʽah-li</rt><rb>一條</rb><rt>ih-diao</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt></ruby>？」<sup>37</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>當該</rb><rt>tông-kæ</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>全副</rb><rt>djün-fu</rt><rb>心腸</rb><rt>sing-dziang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>全副</rb><rt>djün-fu</rt><rb>精神</rb><rt>tsing-jing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>全副</rb><rt>djün-fu</rt><rb>才情</rb><rt>dzæ-dzing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>愛敬</rb><rt>æ-kying</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>。』<sup>38</sup> <ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>頭一個</rb><rt>deo-ih-go</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>大個</rb><rt>do-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>39</sup> <ruby><rb>第二</rb><rt>Di-nyi</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>相像</rb><rt>siang-ziang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>當該</rb><rt>Tông-kæ</rt><rb>愛惜</rb><rt>æ-sih</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>鄰舍家</rb><rt>ling-sô-kô</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>一樣</rb><rt>ih-yiang</rt></ruby>。』 <sup>40</sup> <ruby><rb>攏總</rb><rt>Long-tsong</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>先知人個</rb><rt>sin-cü-nying-go</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>*shih-wô*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>靠着</rb><rt>kʽao-djôh</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>兩條</rb><rt>liang-diao</rt><rb>做主</rb><rt>tso-cü</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>41</sup> <u><ruby><rb>法利賽</rb><rt>Fah-li-sæ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>聚攏東個</rb><rt>jü-long-tong-go</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>問</rb><rt>meng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：<sup>42</sup> 「<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>忖</rb><rt>tsʽeng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>咋個</rb><rt>dza-go</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>誰個</rb><rt>jü-go</rt><rb>後代</rb><rt>ʽEo-dæ</rt></ruby>？」<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb><u>大闢</u>個</rb><rt>Da-bih-go</rt></ruby>。」<sup>43</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>箇末</rb><rt>Keh-meh</rt><rb>咋會</rb><rt>dza-we</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>大闢</rb><rt>Da-bih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>聖靈</rb><rt>*Sing-Ling*</rt><rb>感動</rb><rt>ken-dong</rt><rb>稱呼</rb><rt>tsʽing-hwu</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：<sup>44</sup> 『<ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我個</rb><rt>ngô-go</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>Hao</rt><rb>坐</rb><rt>zo</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>順手邊</rb><rt>jing-siu-pin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等到</rb><rt>teng-tao</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>Ng-go</rt><rb>仇敵</rb><rt>dziu-dih</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>Ng-go</rt><rb>踏腳凳</rb><rt>dah-kyiah-teng</rt></ruby>。』 <sup>45</sup> <ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>Ka-ni</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>大闢</rb><rt>Da-bih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>既然</rb><rt>kyi-jün</rt><rb>稱呼</rb><rt>tsʽing-hwu</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>咋會</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>還</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>大闢</u>個</rb><rt>Da-bih-go</rt><rb>後代</rb><rt>ʽEo-dæ</rt></ruby>？」

<sup>46</sup> <ruby><rb>嘸人</rb><rt>M-nying</rt><rb>能夠</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>一聲</rb><rt>ih-sing</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt><rb>以後</rb><rt>yi-ʽeo</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>嘸人</rb><rt>m-nying</rt><rb>敢</rb><rt>ken</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>問</rb><rt>meng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>。

### Mt. XXIII.

<ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>KEH-GO</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>衆人</rb><rt>cong-nying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>自個</rb><rt>Zi-go</rt><rb>門徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>聽</rb><rt>tʽing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>所以</rb><rt>sô-yi</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>謳</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>保守</rb><rt>pao-siu</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>保守</rb><rt>pao-siu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt><rb>嘸得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>行爲</rb><rt>ʽang-we</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>只</rb><rt>tsih</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>。<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>Nying</rt><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>天</rb><rt>tʽin</rt><rb>罰咒</rb><rt>vah-tsiu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>座位</rb><rt>zo-we</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>連</rb><rt>lin</rt><rb>坐的個</rb><rt>zo-tih-go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>Cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>罰進</rb><rt>vah-tsing</rt><rb>在內</rb><rt>dzæ-nen</rt></ruby>。

<sup>23</sup> 「<ruby><rb>讀書人</rb><rt>Doh-shü-nying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法利賽</rb><rt>Fah-li-sæ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>假好人</rb><rt>kô-hao-nying</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>禍祟</rb><rt>ʽo-se</rt></ruby>！<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>薄荷</rb><rt>bo-ho</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>茴香</rb><rt>we-hyiang</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>芹菜</rb><rt>gying-tsʽæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>十股</rb><rt>jih-kwu</rt><rb>裏頭</rb><rt>li-deo</rt><rb>尊起</rb><rt>tseng-kyʽi</rt><rb>一股</rb><rt>ih-kwu</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>只是</rb><rt>tsih-z</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt><rb>裏向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>頂</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>重頭</rb><rt>djong-deo</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>公義</rb><rt>kong-yi</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>慈悲</rb><rt>dz-pe</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>信行</rb><rt>sing-ʽang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>錯落了</rb><rt>tsʽo-lôh-de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt><rb>蕩樣</rb><rt>dông-yiang</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>該</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>做個</rb><rt>tso-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>箇樣</rb><rt>keh-yiang</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>該</rb><rt>*kæ*</rt><rb>錯落</rb><rt>tsʽo-lôh</rt></ruby>。<sup>24</sup> <ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>領路個</rb><rt>ling-lu-go</rt><rb>瞎子</rb><rt>hah-ts</rt></ruby>！<ruby><rb>香子</rb><rt>hyiang-ts</rt><rb>蟲</rb><rt>djong</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>濾出</rb><rt>li-cʽih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>駱駝</rb><rt>loh-do</rt><rb>倒</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>吞落去</rb><rt>tʽeng-lôh-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>25</sup> 「<ruby><rb>讀書人</rb><rt>Doh-shü-nying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法利賽</rb><rt>Fah-li-sæ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>假好人</rb><rt>kô-hao-nying</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>禍祟</rb><rt>ʽo-se</rt></ruby>！<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>碗盞</rb><rt>un-tsæn</rt><rb>碟子</rb><rt>diah-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>弄</rb><rt>long</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>外頭</rb><rt>nga-deo</rt><rb>乾淨</rb><rt>ken-zing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>裏頭</rb><rt>li-deo</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>強橫霸道</rb><rt>gyiang-wang-pô-dao</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>猛心</rb><rt>mang-sing</rt><rb>𣥼滿了個</rb><rt>tsi-mun-liao-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>26</sup> <ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>瞎眼個</rb><rt>hah-ngæn-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法利賽</rb><rt>Fah-li-sæ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>！<ruby><rb>碗盞</rb><rt>un-tsæn</rt><rb>碟子</rb><rt>diah-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>先</rb><rt>sin</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>弄</rb><rt>long</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>裏頭</rb><rt>li-deo</rt><rb>乾淨</rb><rt>ken-zing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>外頭</rb><rt>nga-deo</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>乾淨了</rb><rt>ken-zing-de</rt></ruby>。

<sup>27</sup> 「<ruby><rb>讀書人</rb><rt>Doh-shü-nying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法利賽</rb><rt>Fah-li-sæ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>假好人</rb><rt>kô-hao-nying</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>禍祟</rb><rt>ʽo-se</rt></ruby>！<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>可比像</rb><rt>kʽo-pi-ziang</rt><rb>粉白了個</rb><rt>feng-bah-liao-go</rt><rb>墳墓</rb><rt>veng-mo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>外頭</rb><rt>nga-deo</rt><rb>看來</rb><rt>kʽen-læ</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>好看個</rb><rt>hao-kʽen-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>裏頭</rb><rt>li-deo</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>屍骨</rb><rt>s-kweh</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>樣樣</rb><rt>yiang-yiang</rt><rb>鏖糟</rb><rt>ao-tsao</rt><rb>𣥼滿了個</rb><rt>tsi-mun-liao-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>28</sup> <ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>外光面</rb><rt>nga-kwông-min</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>正直個</rb><rt>tsing-dzih-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>裏頭</rb><rt>li-deo</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>假意</rb><rt>kô-i</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>非禮</rb><rt>fi-li</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>塞滿了個</rb><rt>seh-mun-liao-go</rt></ruby>。

<sup>29</sup> 「<ruby><rb>讀書人</rb><rt>Doh-shü-nying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法利賽</rb><rt>Fah-li-sæ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>假好人</rb><rt>kô-hao-nying</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>禍祟</rb><rt>ʽo-se</rt></ruby>！<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>去造</rb><rt>kyʽi-zao</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>先知人個</rb><rt>sin-cü-nying-go</rt><rb>墳墓</rb><rt>veng-mo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>裝飾</rb><rt>tsông-sih</rt><rb>正直</rb><rt>tsing-dzih</rt><rb>人個</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>墳頭</rb><rt>veng-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：<sup>30</sup> 『<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>祖宗</rb><rt>tsu-tsong</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>再𣍐</rb><rt>tsæ-ve</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>流出</rb><rt>liu-cʽih</rt><rb>先知人個</rb><rt>sin-cü-nying-go</rt><rb>血</rb><rt>hyüih</rt></ruby>。』<sup>31</sup> <ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>Keh-lah</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>對證</rb><rt>te-tsing</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>弄煞</rb><rt>long-sah</rt><rb>先知人</rb><rt>sin-cü-nying</rt><rb>主顧個</rb><rt>cü-kwu-go</rt><rb>子孫</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt></ruby>。<sup>32</sup> <ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>Ka-ni</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>滿足</rb><rt>mun-tsoh</rt><rb>祖宗個</rb><rt>tsu-tsong-go</rt><rb>惡貫</rb><rt>ôh-kwun</rt></ruby>。<sup>33</sup> <ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>毒蛇</rb><rt>dôh-dzô</rt><rb>生個</rb><rt>sang-go</rt><rb>毒蛇</rb><rt>dôh-dzô</rt></ruby>！<ruby><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>能夠</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>免掉</rb><rt>min-diao</rt><rb>地獄裏個</rb><rt>di-nyüoh-li-go</rt><rb>刑罰</rb><rt>ying-vah</rt></ruby>？<sup>34</sup> <ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>Keh-lah</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>先知人</rb><rt>sin-cü-nying</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>聰明</rb><rt>tsʽong-ming</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>讀書人</rb><rt>doh-shü-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>爾拉個</rb><rt>ng-lah-go</rt><rb>烏碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>有星</rb><rt>yiu-sing</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>殺掉</rb><rt>sah-diao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有星</rb><rt>yiu-sing</rt><rb>十字架</rb><rt>jih-z-kô</rt><rb>釘</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>煞</rb><rt>sah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有星</rb><rt>yiu-sing</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>聚會堂裏</rb><rt>jü-we-dông-li</rt><rb>鞭子</rb><rt>pin-ts</rt><rb>打</rb><rt>tang</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>到處</rb><rt>tao-cʽü</rt><rb>城裏</rb><rt>zing-li</rt><rb>趕</rb><rt>ken</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>走</rb><rt>tseo</rt></ruby>。<sup>35</sup> <ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>世間上</rb><rt>shü-kæn-zông</rt><rb>流出</rb><rt>liu-cʽih</rt><rb>正直</rb><rt>tsing-dzih</rt><rb>人個</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>血</rb><rt>hyüih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>正直個</rb><rt>tsing-dzih-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞伯</rb><rt>Üô-pah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>起頭</rb><rt>kyʽi-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一直</rb><rt>ih-dzih</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>巴拉迦</rb><rt>Pô-lah-kyüô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>撒迦利亞</rb><rt>Sah-kyüô-li-üô</rt></ruby></u>，（<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>祭壇</rb><rt>tsi-dæn</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>大殿個</rb><rt>*da*-din-go</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>殺掉個</rb><rt>sah-diao-go</rt></ruby>，）<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>歸</rb><rt>kwe</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>身上</rb><rt>sing-zông</rt></ruby>。<sup>36</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>真</rb><rt>tsing</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>應着</rb><rt>ing-djôh</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>世代</rb><rt>shü-dæ</rt></ruby>。

<sup>37</sup> 「<u><ruby><rb>耶路撒冷</rb><rt>Yiæ-lu-sah-leng</rt></ruby></u>！<u><ruby><rb>耶路撒冷</rb><rt>Yiæ-lu-sah-leng</rt></ruby></u>！<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>殺掉</rb><rt>sah-diao</rt><rb>先知人</rb><rt>sin-cü-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>烏蕩</rb><rt>u-dông</rt><rb>來個</rb><rt>læ-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>石頭</rb><rt>zah-deo</rt><rb>揞煞</rb><rt>kʽang-sah</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>兒囡</rb><rt>ng-nô</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>多少</rb><rt>to-siao</rt><rb>回數</rb><rt>we-su</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>聚</rb><rt>jü</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>攏來</rb><rt>long-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>雞娘</rb><rt>kyi-nyiang</rt><rb>翼梢</rb><rt>yiah-sao</rt><rb>底下</rb><rt>ti-ʽô</rt><rb>捂</rb><rt>u</rt><rb>小雞</rb><rt>siao-kyi</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>只是</rb><rt>tsih-z</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt></ruby>！<sup>38</sup> <ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>Ka-ni</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾拉個</rb><rt>ng-lah-go</rt><rb>屋落</rb><rt>oh-lô</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>變勒</rb><rt>pin-leh</rt><rb>空地</rb><rt>kʽong-di</rt></ruby>。<sup>39</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>從今以後</rb><rt>Dzong-kying-yi-ʽeo</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一直</rb><rt>ih-dzih</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>靠着</rb><rt>Kʽao-djôh</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>名頭</rb><rt>ming-deo</rt><rb>來個</rb><rt>læ-go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>Cü-kwu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>應該</rb><rt>ing-kæ</rt><rb>祝讚個</rb><rt>coh-tsæn-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>。」

### Mt. XXIV.

<u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>YIÆ-SU</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt><rb>離開</rb><rt>li-kʽæ</rt><rb>聖殿</rb><rt>sing-din</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>門徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>走攏去</rb><rt>tseo-long-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>點點</rb><rt>tin-tin</rt><rb>聖殿個</rb><rt>sing-din-go</rt><rb>屋宇</rb><rt>oh-yü</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt></ruby>。<sup>2</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>攏總</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>真</rb><rt>tsing</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>蕩頭</rb><rt>Dông-deo</rt><rb>將來</rb><rt>tsiang-læ</rt><rb>連只</rb><rt>lin-tsih</rt><rb>一塊</rb><rt>ih-kwʽe</rt><rb>石頭</rb><rt>zah-deo</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>剩落</rb><rt>dzing-lôh</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>石頭</rb><rt>zah-deo</rt><rb>上頂</rb><rt>zông-teng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>攏總</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>拆掉</rb><rt>tsʽah-diao</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>3</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>橄欖</rb><rt>Ken-læn</rt><rb>山</rb><rt>sæn</rt><rb>坐間</rb><rt>zo-kæn</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>門徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>私下</rb><rt>s-ʽô</rt><rb>走攏來</rb><rt>tseo-long-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>問</rb><rt>*meng*</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>*Gyi*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>幾時</rb><rt>Kyi-z</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>話話</rb><rt>wô-wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>時世</rb><rt>z-shü</rt><rb>末個</rb><rt>mah-go</rt><rb>日子</rb><rt>nyih-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>啥個</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>兆頭</rb><rt>ziao-deo</rt></ruby>？」

<sup>4</sup> <sup>9</sup>
