<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>天國個</rb><rt>*tʽin*-koh-go</rt><rb>福音</rb><rt>foh-ing</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>傳開</rb><rt>djün-kʽæ</rt><rb>咸天下</rb><rt>ʽen-tʽin-ʽô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>對證</rb><rt>te-tsing</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>國打國個</rb><rt>koh-tang-koh-go</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>keh-tsao</rt><rb>末個</rb><rt>mah-go</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>來了</rb><rt>læ-de</rt></ruby>。

<sup>15</sup> 「<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>先知</rb><rt>sin-cü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>但以理</rb><rt>Dæn-yi-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>話個</rb><rt>wô-go</rt></ruby>，『<ruby><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>滅掉</rb><rt>mih-diao</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>*di-fông*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>可惡個</rb><rt>kʽo-u-go</rt><rb>東西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt></ruby>，』<ruby><rb>立</rb><rt>lih</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>尊起個</rb><rt>tseng-kyʽi-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>。（<ruby><rb>讀個</rb><rt>doh-go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>該</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>明白</rb><rt>ming-bah</rt></ruby>；）<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>keh-tsao</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>猶太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>來間個</rb><rt>læ-kæn-go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>逃</rb><rt>dao</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>山裏</rb><rt>sæn-li</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>17</sup> <sup>23</sup> 「<ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，『<u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>蕩頭</rb><rt>dông-deo</rt><rb>來東</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt></ruby>。』<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>*wô*</rt></ruby>，『<ruby><rb>箇頭</rb><rt>Keh-deo</rt><rb>來間</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt></ruby>。』<ruby><rb>嘸得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>*gyi*</rt></ruby>。<sup>24</sup> <ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>假</rb><rt>kô</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>假</rb><rt>kô</rt><rb>先知人</rb><rt>sin-cü-nying</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>出來</rb><rt>cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>大大</rb><rt>do-do</rt><rb>神蹟</rb><rt>jing-tsih</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>奇事</rb><rt>gyi-z</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>挑選個</rb><rt>tʽiao-shün-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>帶掉</rb><rt>ta-diao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>帶掉</rb><rt>ta-diao</rt></ruby>。<sup>25</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>預先</rb><rt>yü-sin</rt><rb>話個</rb><rt>wô-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>26</sup> <ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>Ka-ni</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，『<ruby><rb>且看</rb><rt>Tsʽia-kʽen</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>*Kyi-toh*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>曠野</rb><rt>kwʽông-iæ</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>嘸得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>走出去</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>*ʽôh-tsia*</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>*wô*</rt></ruby>，『<ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>Læ</rt><rb>密弗洞風</rb><rt>mih-feh-dong-fong</rt><rb>屋裏</rb><rt>oh-li</rt><rb>來間</rb><rt>*læ-kæn*</rt></ruby>。』<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>嘸得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt></ruby>。<sup>27</sup> <ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>正像</rb><rt>tsing-ziang</rt><rb>閃電</rb><rt>sin-din</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>東邊</rb><rt>tong-pin</rt><rb>發出來</rb><rt>fah-cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>亮過</rb><rt>liang-ko</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>西邊</rb><rt>si-pin</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>人個</rb><rt>Nying-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>Ng-ts</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>是介個</rb><rt>z-ka-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>屍首</rb><rt>s-siu</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>何裏</rb><rt>ʽah-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>老鷹</rb><rt>lao-ing</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>聚攏</rb><rt>jü-long</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>何裏</rb><rt>ʽah-li</rt></ruby>。

<sup>29</sup> 「<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>災難</rb><rt>tsæ-næn</rt><rb>日子</rb><rt>nyih-ts</rt><rb>後頭</rb><rt>ʽeo-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>日頭</rb><rt>nyih-deo</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>黑暗</rb><rt>heh-en</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>月亮</rb><rt>yüih-liang</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>發光</rb><rt>fah-kwông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>星宿</rb><rt>sing-siu</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>天裏</rb><rt>tʽin-li</rt><rb>跌落來</rb><rt>tih-lôh-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>天上</rb><rt>tʽin-zông</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>權柄</rb><rt>gyün-ping</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>搖動</rb><rt>yiao-dong</rt></ruby>。<sup>30</sup> <ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>人個</rb><rt>Nying-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>Ng-ts</rt><rb>兆頭</rb><rt>ziao-deo</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>現出</rb><rt>yin-cʽih</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>天上</rb><rt>tʽin-zông</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>keh-tsao</rt><rb>天下個</rb><rt>tʽin-ʽô-go</rt><rb>衆</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>支派</rb><rt>ts-pʽa</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>哭勒</rb><rt>kʽoh-leh</rt><rb>傷心</rb><rt>sông-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>人個</rb><rt>Nying-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>Ng-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>大大</rb><rt>do-do</rt><rb>權柄</rb><rt>gyün-ping</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>榮華</rb><rt>yüong-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>坐勒</rb><rt>zo-leh</rt><rb>天裏個</rb><rt>tʽin-li-go</rt><rb>雲</rb><rt>yüing</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。<sup>31</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>天使</rb><rt>tʽin-s</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>號筒個</rb><rt>ʽao-dong-go</rt><rb>大大</rb><rt>do-do</rt><rb>聲響</rb><rt>sing-hyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>天下</rb><rt>tʽin-ʽô</rt><rb>蕩邊</rb><rt>dông-pin</rt><rb>盡頭</rb><rt>zing-deo</rt><rb>一直</rb><rt>ih-dzih</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>箇邊</rb><rt>keh-pin</rt><rb>盡頭</rb><rt>zing-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>聚攏</rb><rt>jü-long</rt><rb>四方</rb><rt>s-fông</rt><rb>挑選個</rb><rt>tʽiao-shün-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。

<sup>32</sup> 「<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt><rb>無花果</rb><rt>vu-hwô-ko</rt><rb>樹</rb><rt>jü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>忖</rb><rt>tsʽeng</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>比方</rb><rt>pi-fông</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>椏枝</rb><rt>ô-ts</rt><rb>嫩起來</rb><rt>neng-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>葉爿</rb><rt>yih-bæn</rt><rb>爆出來</rb><rt>pao-cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>夏天</rb><rt>ʽô-tʽin</rt><rb>快</rb><rt>kwʽa</rt><rb>到了</rb><rt>tao-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>33</sup> <ruby><rb>是介個</rb><rt>Z-ka-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>樣樣</rb><rt>yiang-yiang</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>神明個</rb><rt>*Jing-ming-go*</rt><rb>國</rb><rt>*koh*</rt></ruby><sup> </sup><ruby><rb>快</rb><rt>kwʽa</rt><rb>到了</rb><rt>tao-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>門口</rb><rt>meng-kʽeo</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>。<sup>34</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>真</rb><rt>tsing</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>世代</rb><rt>shü-dæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>等到</rb><rt>teng-tao</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>做成</rb><rt>tso-dzing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>過去</rb><rt>ko-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>35</sup> <ruby><rb>天</rb><rt>Tʽin</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>過去</rb><rt>ko-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>我個</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>過去</rb><rt>ko-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>42</sup> 「<ruby><rb>所以</rb><rt>Sô-yi</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>警醒</rb><rt>kying-sing</rt></ruby>：<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>爾拉個</rb><rt>ng-lah-go</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>啥個</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>來其</rb><rt>læ-gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>43</sup> <ruby><rb>家主</rb><rt>Kô-cü</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>賊</rb><rt>zeh</rt><rb>何裏</rb><rt>ʽah-li</rt><rb>一更</rb><rt>ih-kang</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>警醒</rb><rt>kying-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>再𣍐</rb><rt>tsæ-ve</rt><rb>謳</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>屋落</rb><rt>oh-lô</rt><rb>拆</rb><rt>tsʽah</rt><rb>洞</rb><rt>dong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>曉得個</rb><rt>hyiao-teh-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>44</sup> <ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>Keh-lah</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>當該</rb><rt>tông-kæ</rt><rb>防備好</rb><rt>bông-be-hao</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>人個</rb><rt>Nying-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>Ng-ts</rt><rb>來個</rb><rt>læ-go</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>忖弗到個</rb><rt>tsʽeng-feh-tao-go</rt></ruby>。

### Mt. XXV.

<sup>13</sup> 「<ruby><rb>所以</rb><rt>Sô-yi</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>警醒</rb><rt>kying-sing</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>人個</rb><rt>Nying-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>Ng-ts</rt><rb>啥個</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>日子</rb><rt>nyih-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>啥個</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>時辰</rb><rt>z-zing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。

<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>有個</rb><rt>yiu-go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>還</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>加湊</rb><rt>kô-tsʽeo</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>有餘</rb><rt>yiu-yü</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>嘸得個</rb><rt>m̆-teh-go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>連</rb><rt>lin</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>有個</rb><rt>yiu-go</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>奪勒去</rb><rt>deh-leh-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>30</sup> <ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>Wa-yiu</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>嘸用個</rb><rt>m-yüong-go</rt><rb>奴僕</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt><rb>趕出</rb><rt>ken-cʽih</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>外頭</rb><rt>nga-deo</rt><rb>黑暗個</rb><rt>heh-en-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇頭</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>咬勒</rb><rt>ngao-leh</rt><rb>牙齒</rb><rt>ngô-tsʽ</rt><rb>啼哭個</rb><rt>di-kʽoh-go</rt><rb>聲響</rb><rt>sing-hyiang</rt></ruby>。』

<sup>31</sup> 「<ruby><rb>人個</rb><rt>NYING-GO</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>Ng-ts</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>榮華</rb><rt>yüong-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>帶勒</rb><rt>ta-leh</rt><rb>攏總</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>聖善個</rb><rt>sing-jün-go</rt><rb>天使</rb><rt>tʽin-s</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>坐</rb><rt>zo</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>榮華個</rb><rt>yüong-wô-go</rt><rb>座位</rb><rt>zo-we</rt></ruby>。<sup>32</sup> <ruby><rb>萬國個</rb><rt>Væn-koh-go</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>聚攏</rb><rt>jü-long</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>分出</rb><rt>feng-cʽih</rt><rb>兩號生</rb><rt>liang-ʽao-sang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>正像</rb><rt>tsing-ziang</rt><rb>看羊</rb><rt>kʽen-yiang</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>分出</rb><rt>feng-cʽih</rt><rb>湖羊</rb><rt>wu-yiang</rt><rb>山羊</rb><rt>sæn-yiang</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>湖羊</rb><rt>wu-yiang</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>順手邊</rb><rt>jing-siu-pin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>山羊</rb><rt>sæn-yiang</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>借手邊</rb><rt>tsia-siu-pin</rt></ruby>。

<sup>34</sup> 「<ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>Keh-tsao</rt><rb>皇帝</rb><rt>Wông-ti</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>順手邊</rb><rt>jing-siu-pin</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>Ah-tia</rt><rb>祝福個</rb><rt>coh-foh-go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>接受</rb><rt>tsih-ziu</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>開闢</rb><rt>kʽæ-bih</rt><rb>天地</rb><rt>tʽin-di</rt><rb>備好東個</rb><rt>be-hao-tong-go</rt><rb>國</rb><rt>koh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>產業</rb><rt>tsʽæn-nyih</rt></ruby>。<sup>35</sup> <ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>肚飢</rb><rt>du-kyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>喫</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>口渴</rb><rt>kʽeo-kʽeh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>喝</rb><rt>hah</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>出門間</rb><rt>cʽih-meng-kæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>收留</rb><rt>siu-liu</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>；<sup>36</sup> <ruby><rb>出膊個</rb><rt>cʽih-poh-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>穿</rb><rt>cʽün</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt><rb>病</rb><rt>bing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>顧着</rb><rt>kwu-djôh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>落</rb><rt>lôh</rt><rb>牢監</rb><rt>lao-kæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>看顧</rb><rt>kʽen-kwu</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>。』<sup>37</sup> <ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>正直</rb><rt>tsing-dzih</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>應起</rb><rt>ing-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>幾時</rb><rt>kyi-z</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>肚飢</rb><rt>du-kyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>喫</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>口渴</rb><rt>kʽeo-kʽeh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>喝</rb><rt>hah</rt></ruby>？<sup>38</sup> <ruby><rb>幾時</rb><rt>Kyi-z</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>出門間</rb><rt>cʽih-meng-kæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>收留</rb><rt>siu-liu</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>出膊個</rb><rt>cʽih-poh-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>穿</rb><rt>cʽün</rt></ruby>？<sup>39</sup> <ruby><rb>幾時</rb><rt>Kyi-z</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt><rb>病</rb><rt>bing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>落</rb><rt>lôh</rt><rb>牢監</rb><rt>lao-kæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>看顧</rb><rt>kʽen-kwu</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt></ruby>？』<sup>40</sup> <ruby><rb>皇帝</rb><rt>Wông-ti</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>應起</rb><rt>ing-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>真</rb><rt>tsing</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>頂</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>小</rb><rt>siao</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>多少</rb><rt>to-siao</rt><rb>好處</rb><rt>hao-cʽü</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>*yiu*</rt><rb>多少</rb><rt>*to-siao*</rt><rb>好處</rb><rt>*hao-cʽü*</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>。』

<sup>41</sup> 「<ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>Keh-tsao</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>借手邊</rb><rt>tsia-siu-pin</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>咒罵</rb><rt>tsiu-mô</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>離開</rb><rt>li-kʽæ</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>魔鬼</rb><rt>mo-kwe</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>差使</rb><rt>tsʽa-s</rt><rb>備好間個</rb><rt>be-hao-kæn-go</rt><rb>永遠</rb><rt>üong-yün</rt><rb>火裏</rb><rt>ho-li</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>42</sup> <ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>肚飢</rb><rt>du-kyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m̆-teh</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>喫</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>口渴</rb><rt>kʽeo-kʽeh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m̆-teh</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>喝</rb><rt>hah</rt></ruby>；<sup>43</sup> <ruby><rb>出門間</rb><rt>cʽih-meng-kæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m̆-teh</rt><rb>收留</rb><rt>siu-liu</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>出膊個</rb><rt>cʽih-poh-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m̆-teh</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>穿</rb><rt>cʽün</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt><rb>病</rb><rt>bing</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>落</rb><rt>lôh</rt><rb>牢監</rb><rt>lao-kæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m̆-teh</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>*læ*</rt><rb>看顧</rb><rt>kʽen-kwu</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>。』<sup>44</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>應起</rb><rt>ing-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>幾時</rb><rt>kyi-z</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>肚飢</rb><rt>du-kyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>口渴</rb><rt>kʽeo-kʽeh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>出門間</rb><rt>cʽih-meng-kæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>出膊個</rb><rt>cʽih-poh-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt><rb>病</rb><rt>bing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>落</rb><rt>lôh</rt><rb>牢監</rb><rt>lao-kæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m̆-teh</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>*læ*</rt><rb>供應</rb><rt>kong-ing</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt></ruby>？』<sup>45</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>應起</rb><rt>ing-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>真</rb><rt>tsing</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>頂</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>小</rb><rt>siao</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>多少</rb><rt>to-siao</rt><rb>好處</rb><rt>hao-cʽü</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m̆-teh</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>*yiu*</rt><rb>多少</rb><rt>*to-siao*</rt><rb>好處</rb><rt>*hao-cʽü*</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m̆-teh</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>。<sup>46</sup> <ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>永遠</rb><rt>üong-yün</rt><rb>刑罰裏</rb><rt>ying-vah-li</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>正直</rb><rt>tsing-dzih</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>永遠</rb><rt>üong-yün</rt><rb>活命</rb><rt>weh-ming</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>*kyʽi*</rt></ruby>。』」

### Mt. XXVI.

<u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>YIÆ-SU</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>講好</rb><rt>kông-hao</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>攏總</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>門徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：<sup>2</sup> 「<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>兩</rb><rt>liang</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>逾越節</rb><rt>Yü-yüih-tsih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>人個</rb><rt>Nying-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>Ng-ts</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>送</rb><rt>song</rt><rb>官</rb><rt>*kwun*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>釘</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>十字架上</rb><rt>jih-z-kô-zông</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>祭司頭</rb><rt>tsi-s-deo</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>讀書人</rb><rt>doh-shü-nying</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>百姓個</rb><rt>pah-sing-go</rt><rb>長老</rb><rt>tsiang-lao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>聚集攏</rb><rt>jü-jih-long</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>祭司頭</rb><rt>tsi-s-deo</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>該亞法</u>個</rb><rt>Kæ-üô-fah-go</rt><rb>衙門裏</rb><rt>ngô-meng-li</rt></ruby>，<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>商量</rb><rt>siang-liang</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>計較</rb><rt>kyi-kao</rt><rb>抲</rb><rt>kʽô</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>弄煞</rb><rt>long-sah</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>只是</rb><rt>Tsih-z</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>嘸得好</rb><rt>M-nao</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>節肯</rb><rt>tsih-kʽeng</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>恐怕</rb><rt>kʽong-pʽô</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>鬧事</rb><rt>nao-z</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>6</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>YIÆ-SU</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>伯大尼</rb><rt>Pah-da-nyi</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt><rb>大麻風</rb><rt>da-mô-fong</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>西門</rb><rt>Si-meng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>屋裏</rb><rt>oh-li</rt><rb>來間</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt></ruby>。<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt><rb>馱勒</rb><rt>do-leh</rt><rb>頂</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>貴</rb><rt>kyü</rt><rb>香油</rb><rt>hyiang-yiu</rt><rb>𣥼的個</rb><rt>tsi-tih-go</rt><rb>玉個</rb><rt>nyüoh-go</rt><rb>盒子</rb><rt>ʽeh-ts</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>坐席個</rb><rt>zo-zih-go</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>走攏來</rb><rt>tseo-long-læ</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>頭裏</rb><rt>deo-li</rt></ruby>。<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>門徒</rb><rt>Meng-du</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>怏了</rb><rt>ông-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>糜費掉</rb><rt>me-fi-diao</rt><rb>作</rb><rt>tsôh</rt><rb>啥</rb><rt>soh</rt></ruby>？<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>香油</rb><rt>hyiang-yiu</rt><rb>弗然</rb><rt>feh-jün</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>賣</rb><rt>ma</rt><rb>許多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>銅鈿</rb><rt>*dong-din*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>分</rb><rt>feng</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>窮人</rb><rt>gyüong-nying</rt></ruby>。」<sup>10</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>得知</rb><rt>teh-cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>咋會</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>弄</rb><rt>long</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt><rb>擔</rb><rt>tæn</rt><rb>心事</rb><rt>sing-z</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>做個</rb><rt>tso-go</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>一樣</rb><rt>ih-yiang</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>。<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>窮人</rb><rt>gyüong-nying</rt><rb>長通</rb><rt>dziang-tʽong</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>來東</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m̆-teh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>長通</rb><rt>dziang-tʽong</rt><rb>來東</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt></ruby>。<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>香油</rb><rt>hyiang-yiu</rt><rb>倒</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>身上</rb><rt>sing-zông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>喪事</rb><rt>sông-z</rt><rb>做個</rb><rt>tso-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>真</rb><rt>tsing</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>咸天下</rb><rt>ʽEn-tʽin-ʽô</rt><rb>弗論</rb><rt>feh-leng</rt><rb>啥個</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>福音</rb><rt>foh-ing</rt><rb>傳開個</rb><rt>djün-kʽæ-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>講到</rb><rt>kông-tao</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>做個</rb><rt>tso-go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt><rb>記得</rb><rt>kyi-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>十二</rb><rt>jih-nyi</rt><rb>門徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>裏向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>加略</u>個</rb><rt>Kyüô-liah-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>猶大</rb><rt>Yiu-da</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>走</rb><rt>tseo</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>祭司頭個</rb><rt>tsi-s-deo-go</rt><rb>烏碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：<sup>15</sup> 「<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>肯</rb><rt>kʽeng</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>多少</rb><rt>to-siao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>將</rb><rt>tsiang</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>賣</rb><rt>ma</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>？」<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>稱</rb><rt>tsʽing</rt><rb>三十塊</rb><rt>sæn-jih-kwʽe</rt><rb>銀子</rb><rt>nying-ts</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>16</sup> <u><ruby><rb>猶大</rb><rt>*Yiu-da*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>從此之後</rb><rt>dzong-tsʽ-ts-ʽeo</rt><rb>尋</rb><rt>zing</rt><rb>機會</rb><rt>kyi-we</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>交代</rb><rt>kao-dæ</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>。

<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>無酵節個</rb><rt>VU-KAO-TSIH-GO</rt><rb>頭日</rb><rt>deo-nyih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>門徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>走攏</rb><rt>tseo-long</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>耶穌</u>個</rb><rt>Yiæ-su-go</rt><rb>身邊</rb><rt>sing-pin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>何裏</rb><rt>ʽah-li</rt><rb>備辦</rb><rt>be-bæn</rt><rb>逾越</rb><rt>yü-yüih</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>喫</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt></ruby>？」<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>城裏</rb><rt>zing-li</rt><rb>某人</rb><rt>mo-nying</rt><rb>屋裏</rb><rt>*oh-li*</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>先生</rb><rt>Sin-sang</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我個</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>近了</rb><rt>gying-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>門徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>屋裏</rb><rt>oh-li</rt><rb>過</rb><rt>ko</rt><rb>逾越節</rb><rt>Yü-yüih-tsih</rt></ruby>。』」<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>門徒</rb><rt>Meng-du</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>依</rb><rt>i</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>逾越</rb><rt>yü-yüih</rt><rb>備好間</rb><rt>be-hao-kæn</rt></ruby>。

<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>Tao</rt><rb>夜快</rb><rt>yia-kwʽa</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>十二</rb><rt>jih-nyi</rt><rb>門徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>坐席</rb><rt>zo-zih</rt></ruby>。<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>喫個</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-go</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>真</rb><rt>tsing</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>賣掉</rb><rt>ma-diao</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>。」<sup>22</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>大樣</rb><rt>do-nyiang</rt><rb>憂悶</rb><rt>iu-meng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>個打個</rb><rt>ko-tang-ko</rt><rb>問</rb><rt>meng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt></ruby>？」<sup>23</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>Teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>盆子裏</rb><rt>beng-ts-li</rt><rb>下手個</rb><rt>ʽô-siu-go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>賣掉</rb><rt>ma-diao</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>。<sup>24</sup> <ruby><rb>人個</rb><rt>Nying-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>Ng-ts</rt><rb>原是</rb><rt>nyün-z</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>書裏</rb><rt>*Shü-li*</rt><rb>寫落</rb><rt>sia-lôh</rt><rb>指點</rb><rt>ts-tin</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>介個</rb><rt>ka-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt><rb>賣掉</rb><rt>ma-diao</rt><rb>人個</rb><rt>Nying-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>Ng-ts</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>禍祟</rb><rt>ʽo-se</rt></ruby>！<ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt><rb>還是</rb><rt>wa-z</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>便宜</rb><rt>bin-i</rt></ruby>。」<sup>25</sup> <ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>Iao</rt><rb>賣掉</rb><rt>ma-diao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>猶大</rb><rt>Yiu-da</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>應起</rb><rt>ing-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>夫子</rb><rt>Fu-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt></ruby>？」<u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>話着了</rb><rt>wô-djôh-de</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>26</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>喫個</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-go</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>馱勒</rb><rt>do-leh</rt><rb>饅頭</rb><rt>mun-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>祝福仔</rb><rt>coh-foh-ts</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>，<ruby><rb>𤖼開</rb><rt>pʽah-kʽæ</rt><rb>交付</rb><rt>kao-fu</rt><rb>門徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>馱勒</rb><rt>do-leh</rt><rb>喫</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>我個</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>肌身</rb><rt>kyi-sing</rt></ruby>。」<sup>27</sup> <ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>Yi</rt><rb>馱勒</rb><rt>do-leh</rt><rb>杯子</rb><rt>pe-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>祝謝仔</rb><rt>coh-zia-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>交付</rb><rt>kao-fu</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>衆人</rb><rt>cong-nying</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>喝</rb><rt>hah</rt></ruby>，<sup>28</sup> <ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>我個</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>血</rb><rt>hyüih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>新約個</rb><rt>sing-iah-go</rt><rb>血</rb><rt>hyüih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>许多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>流出來</rb><rt>liu-cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>赦掉</rb><rt>sô-diao</rt><rb>罪</rb><rt>ze</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>。<sup>29</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>從今以後</rb><rt>Dzong-kying-yi-ʽeo</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>喫</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>葡萄</rb><rt>bu-dao</rt><rb>酒</rb><rt>tsiu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一直</rb><rt>ih-dzih</rt><rb>等到</rb><rt>teng-tao</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>阿爹個</rb><rt>Ah-tia-go</rt><rb>國裏</rb><rt>koh-li</rt><rb>喫</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt><rb>新</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>酒</rb><rt>tsiu</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>\[1\]*Yiu-teh* *Hyi-lah* *kwu-kyün* *wô*, *coh-zia-ts*: *kʽen* Mk. 6. 41. 有得<u>希臘</u>古卷話，祝謝仔。看 Mk. 6. 41.（原註）</sup>

<sup>30</sup> <ruby><rb>聖詩</rb><rt>Sing-s</rt></ruby><sup> </sup><ruby><rb>唱好</rb><rt>tsʽông-hao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>橄欖</rb><rt>Ken-læn</rt><rb>山</rb><rt>sæn</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>Keh-tsao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：<sup>31</sup> 「<ruby><rb>今末</rb><rt>Kyih-mih</rt><rb>夜到</rb><rt>yia-tao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>衆人</rb><rt>cong-nying</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>厭憎</rb><rt>in-tseng</rt><rb>棄掉</rb><rt>kyʽi-diao</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>書裏</rb><rt>*Shü-li*</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>寫落的</rb><rt>sia-lôh-tih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>打</rb><rt>tang</rt><rb>看羊個</rb><rt>Kʽen-yiang-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>Nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇潮</rb><rt>keh-dziao</rt><rb>羊</rb><rt>yiang</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>散開</rb><rt>sæn-kʽæ</rt></ruby>。』 <sup>32</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>活轉來</rb><rt>weh-cün-læ</rt><rb>後頭</rb><rt>ʽeo-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>先如</rb><rt>sin-jü</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>加利利</rb><rt>Kyüô-li-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。」<sup>33</sup> <u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>Ziu-z</rt><rb>衆人</rb><rt>cong-nying</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>厭憎</rb><rt>in-tseng</rt><rb>棄掉</rb><rt>kyʽi-diao</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>永</rb><rt>üong</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>厭憎</rb><rt>in-tseng</rt><rb>棄掉</rb><rt>kyʽi-diao</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>*Ng*</rt></ruby>。」<sup>34</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>真</rb><rt>tsing</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>今末</rb><rt>Kyih-mih</rt><rb>夜到</rb><rt>yia-tao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>雞</rb><rt>kyi</rt><rb>弗曾</rb><rt>feh-zing</rt><rb>啼個</rb><rt>di-go</rt><rb>前頭</rb><rt>zin-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>三遭</rb><rt>sæn-tsao</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>招認</rb><rt>tsiao-jing</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>。」<sup>35</sup> <u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>再𣍐</rb><rt>tsæ-ve</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>招認</rb><rt>tsiao-jing</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>衆</rb><rt>Cong</rt><rb>門徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\]*ʽÔh-tsia* *wô*, tsæn-me-s. 或者話，讚美詩。（原註）</sup>

<sup>36</sup> <ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>KEH-TSAO</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>一𡍲</rb><rt>ih-tʽah</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>客西馬尼</rb><rt>Kʽeh-si-mô-nyi</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>門徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>箇頭</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>禱告</rb><rt>tao-kao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>坐勒</rb><rt>zo-leh</rt><rb>蕩頭</rb><rt>dông-deo</rt><rb>等東</rb><rt>teng-tong</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>37</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>Ziu</rt><rb>帶勒</rb><rt>ta-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>西庇太</u>個</rb><rt>Si-pi-tʽa-go</rt><rb>兩</rb><rt>liang</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>心裏</rb><rt>*sing-li*</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>憂悶</rb><rt>iu-meng</rt><rb>悲傷起來了</rb><rt>pe-shông-kyʽi-læ-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>38</sup> <ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>Keh-tsao</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我個</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>大樣</rb><rt>do-nyiang</rt><rb>憂悶</rb><rt>iu-meng</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>死個</rb><rt>si-go</rt><rb>田地</rb><rt>din-di</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>蕩頭</rb><rt>dông-deo</rt><rb>來東</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>警醒</rb><rt>kying-sing</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>39</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>Ziu</rt><rb>進去</rb><rt>tsing-kyʽi</rt><rb>一眼</rb><rt>ih-ngæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>撲倒</rb><rt>pʽoh-tao</rt><rb>求求</rb><rt>gyiu-gyiu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我個</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>Ah-tia</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>好使得</rb><rt>hao-s-teh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>杯子</rb><rt>pe-ts</rt><rb>離開</rb><rt>li-kʽæ</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>雖即</rb><rt>se-tsih</rt><rb>介個</rb><rt>ka-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m̆-teh</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>隨</rb><rt>ze</rt><rb>我個</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>主意</rb><rt>cü-i</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>總</rb><rt>tsong</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>隨</rb><rt>ze</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>Ng-go</rt><rb>主意</rb><rt>*cü-i*</rt></ruby>。」<sup>40</sup> <ruby><rb>走</rb><rt>Tseo</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>門徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>烏碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>睏熟的</rb><rt>kwʽeng-joh-tih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>能夠</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>警醒</rb><rt>kying-sing</rt><rb>一</rb><rt>ih</rt><rb>點鐘</rb><rt>tin-cong</rt><rb>工夫</rb><rt>kong-fu</rt><rb>嗎</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<sup>41</sup> <ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>Yüong</rt><rb>警醒</rb><rt>kying-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>禱告</rb><rt>tao-kao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>省勒</rb><rt>sæn-leh</rt><rb>走進</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>迷惑</rb><rt>mi-ʽôh</rt><rb>裏向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>靈性</rb><rt>Ling-sing</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>情願個</rb><rt>dzing-nyün-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>只是</rb><rt>tsih-z</rt><rb>肉身</rb><rt>nyüoh-sing</rt><rb>軟弱</rb><rt>nyün-ziah</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>42</sup> <ruby><rb>第二伐</rb><rt>Di-nyi-vah</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>禱告</rb><rt>tao-kao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我個</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>Ah-tia</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>必</rb><rt>pih</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>喫</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>杯子</rb><rt>pe-ts</rt></ruby>，——<ruby><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>離開</rb><rt>li-kʽæ</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>使弗得個</rb><rt>s-feh-teh-go</rt></ruby>，——<ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>ka-ni</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>Ng-go</rt><rb>旨意</rb><rt>ts-i</rt><rb>做成</rb><rt>tso-dzing</rt></ruby>。」<sup>43</sup> <ruby><rb>走來</rb><rt>Tseo-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>睏熟的</rb><rt>kwʽeng-joh-tih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>眼睛</rb><rt>ngæn-tsing</rt><rb>眯攏去了</rb><rt>mi-long-kyʽi-de</rt></ruby>。

<sup>44</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>Ziu</rt><rb>離開</rb><rt>li-kʽæ</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>第三遭</rb><rt>di-sæn-tsao</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>禱告</rb><rt>tao-kao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話法</rb><rt>wô-fah</rt></ruby>。<sup>45</sup> <ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>Keh-tsao</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>門徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>烏碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>以後</rb><rt>Yi-ʽeo</rt><rb>隨</rb><rt>ze</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>睏熟</rb><rt>kwʽeng-joh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>將息</rb><rt>tsiang-sih</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>近了</rb><rt>gying-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>人個</rb><rt>Nying-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>Ng-ts</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>交代</rb><rt>kao-dæ</rt><rb>罪人個</rb><rt>ze-nying-go</rt><rb>手裏</rb><rt>siu-li</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>。<sup>46</sup> <ruby><rb>爬起</rb><rt>Bô-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>去了</rb><rt>kyʽi-de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>賣</rb><rt>ma</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>近攏來了</rb><rt>gying-long-læ-de</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>47</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>GYI</rt><rb>還</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>講個</rb><rt>kông-go</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>十二</rb><rt>jih-nyi</rt><rb>門徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>裏向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>*kyiao-leh*</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>猶大</rb><rt>Yiu-da</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>來了</rb><rt>læ-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>一大潮</rb><rt>ih-do-dziao</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>帶勒</rb><rt>ta-leh</rt><rb>刀</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>棍子</rb><rt>kweng-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>祭司頭</rb><rt>tsi-s-deo</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>百姓個</rb><rt>pah-sing-go</rt><rb>長老</rb><rt>tsiang-lao</rt><rb>烏碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>猶大</rb><rt>Yiu-da</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。<sup>48</sup> <ruby><rb>賣</rb><rt>Ma</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>掉個</rb><rt>diao-go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>暗號</rb><rt>en-ʽao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>嗅嘴個</rb><rt>hyüong-cü-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>抲</rb><rt>kʽô</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>。」<sup>49</sup> <ruby><rb>隨手</rb><rt>Ze-siu</rt><rb>走攏</rb><rt>tseo-long</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>耶穌</u>個</rb><rt>Yiæ-su-go</rt><rb>身邊</rb><rt>sing-pin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>夫子</rb><rt>Fu-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>請</rb><rt>tsʽing</rt><rb>安</rb><rt>en</rt></ruby>！」<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>嗅嘴</rb><rt>hyüong-cü</rt></ruby>。<sup>50</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>朋友</rb><rt>Beng-yiu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>爲</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>啥個</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>？」<ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>Keh-tsao</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>走攏來</rb><rt>tseo-long-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>下手</rb><rt>ʽô-siu</rt><rb>抲</rb><rt>kʽô</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u>。

<sup>51</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>身邊</rb><rt>sing-pin</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>動手</rb><rt>dong-siu</rt><rb>拔出</rb><rt>bah-cʽih</rt><rb>刀</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>斬</rb><rt>tsæn</rt><rb>祭司頭個</rb><rt>tsi-s-deo-go</rt><rb>奴僕</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>削落</rb><rt>siah-lôh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>耳朵</rb><rt>ng-to</rt></ruby>。<sup>52</sup> <ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>Keh-tsao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾個</rb><rt>Ng-go</rt><rb>刀</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>收進</rb><rt>siu-tsing</rt><rb>刀殼裏</rb><rt>tao-kʽôh-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>馱勒</rb><rt>do-leh</rt><rb>刀</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>斬個</rb><rt>*tsæn-go*</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>刀</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>斬煞</rb><rt>tsæn-sah</rt></ruby>。<sup>53</sup> <ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>倒</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>忖</rb><rt>tsʽeng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>現在</rb><rt>yin-dzæ</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>求</rb><rt>gyiu</rt><rb>我個</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>Ah-tia</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>多如</rb><rt>to-jü</rt><rb>十二</rb><rt>jih-nyi</rt><rb>營</rb><rt>ying</rt><rb>天使</rb><rt>tʽin-s</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>幫助</rb><rt>pông-dzu</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>嗎</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>！<sup>54</sup> <ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>Ziah-z</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>聖書</rb><rt>Sing-shü</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>有個</rb><rt>yiu-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>*keh-go*</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>*shih-wô*</rt><rb>咋會</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>應勒來</rb><rt>ing-leh-læ</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？」

<sup>55</sup> <ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>箇潮</rb><rt>keh-dziao</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>走出來</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>咋會</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>馱勒</rb><rt>do-leh</rt><rb>刀</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>棍子</rb><rt>kweng-ts</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>抲</rb><rt>kʽô</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>抲</rb><rt>kʽô</rt><rb>強盜</rb><rt>gyiang-dao</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>日日</rb><rt>nyih-nyih</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>聖殿裏</rb><rt>sing-din-li</rt><rb>坐勒</rb><rt>zo-leh</rt><rb>爾拉個</rb><rt>ng-lah-go</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>抲</rb><rt>kʽô</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>。<sup>56</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>做法</rb><rt>tso-fah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>先知人</rb><rt>sin-cü-nying</rt><rb>書裏個</rb><rt>shü-li-go</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>效驗</rb><rt>yiao-nyiæn</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>Keh-tsao</rt><rb>門徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>離開</rb><rt>li-kʽæ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>逃走了</rb><rt>dao-tseo-de</rt></ruby>。

<sup>57</sup> <ruby><rb>抲</rb><rt>Kʽô</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>帶勒</rb><rt>ta-leh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>祭司頭</rb><rt>tsi-s-deo</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>該亞法</u>個</rb><rt>Kæ-üô-fah-go</rt><rb>衙門裏</rb><rt>*ngô-meng-li*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>讀書人</rb><rt>doh-shü-nying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>長老</rb><rt>tsiang-lao</rt><rb>聚攏間</rb><rt>jü-long-kæn</rt><rb>烏碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt></ruby>。<sup>58</sup> <u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>遠遠</rb><rt>yün-yün</rt><rb>跟勒</rb><rt>keng-leh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>一直</rb><rt>ih-dzih</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>祭司頭</rb><rt>tsi-s-deo</rt><rb>衙門裏</rb><rt>ngô-meng-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>走進</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>裏向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>差人</rb><rt>tsʽa-nying</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>坐落</rb><rt>zo-lôh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>看看</rb><rt>kʽen-kʽen</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>*z-ken*</rt><rb>咋個</rb><rt>dza-go</rt><rb>結局</rb><rt>kyih-gyüoh</rt></ruby>。

<sup>59</sup> <ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>祭司頭</rb><rt>tsi-s-deo</rt><rb>長老</rb><rt>tsiang-lao</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>全</rb><rt>djün</rt><rb>公會</rb><rt>kong-we</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>尋</rb><rt>zing</rt><rb>假</rb><rt>kô</rt><rb>對證</rb><rt>te-tsing</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>告</rb><rt>kao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>辦</rb><rt>bæn</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>死罪</rb><rt>si-ze</rt></ruby>，<sup>60</sup> <ruby><rb>只是</rb><rt>tsih-z</rt><rb>尋弗着</rb><rt>zing-feh-djôh</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>雖即</rb><rt>se-tsih</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>許多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>假</rb><rt>kô</rt><rb>對證</rb><rt>te-tsing</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>仍舊</rb><rt>dzing-gyiu</rt><rb>尋弗着</rb><rt>zing-feh-djôh</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>後頭</rb><rt>ʽEo-deo</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>兩個</rb><rt>liang-go</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>假</rb><rt>kô</rt><rb>對證</rb><rt>te-tsing</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>能夠</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>毀掉</rb><rt>hwe-diao</rt><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>殿</rb><rt>din</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>三</rb><rt>sæn</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt><rb>裏頭</rb><rt>li-deo</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>起</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt></ruby>。』」

<sup>62</sup> <ruby><rb>祭司頭</rb><rt>Tsi-s-deo</rt><rb>立起來</rb><rt>lih-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>一句</rb><rt>ih-kyü</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>嗎</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>對證</rb><rt>te-tsing</rt><rb>告</rb><rt>kao</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>到底</rb><rt>tao-ti</rt><rb>咋個</rb><rt>dza-go</rt></ruby>？」<sup>63</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>響</rb><rt>hyiang</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>祭司頭</rb><rt>Tsi-s-deo</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>開</rb><rt>kʽæ</rt><rb>口</rb><rt>kʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>指點</rb><rt>ts-tin</rt><rb>箇位</rb><rt>keh-we</rt><rb>活個</rb><rt>weh-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>罰咒</rb><rt>vah-tsiu</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>到底</rb><rt>tao-ti</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>Ng-ts</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt></ruby>？」<sup>64</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>話着了</rb><rt>wô-djôh-de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>還有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>從今以後</rb><rt>Dzong-kying-yi-ʽeo</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>人個</rb><rt>Nying-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>Ng-ts</rt><rb>坐</rb><rt>zo</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>能幹</rb><rt>neng-ken</rt><rb>主顧個</rb><rt>Cü-kwu-go</rt><rb>順手邊</rb><rt>jing-siu-pin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>成勒</rb><rt>dzing-leh</rt><rb>天裏個</rb><rt>tʽin-li-go</rt><rb>雲</rb><rt>yüing</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>65</sup> <ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>Keh-tsao</rt><rb>祭司頭</rb><rt>tsi-s-deo</rt><rb>扯開</rb><rt>cʽô-kʽæ</rt><rb>自個</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>衣裳</rb><rt>i-zông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>褻瀆個</rb><rt>sih-doh-go</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>何用</rb><rt>ʽo-yüong</rt><rb>別人個</rb><rt>bih-nying-go</rt><rb>對證</rb><rt>te-tsing</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>褻瀆個</rb><rt>sih-doh-go</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>難間</rb><rt>næn-kæn</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>聽聞了</rb><rt>tʽing-meng-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>66</sup> <ruby><rb>爾拉個</rb><rt>Ng-lah-go</rt><rb>意思</rb><rt>i-s</rt><rb>咋個</rb><rt>dza-go</rt></ruby>？」<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>犯着</rb><rt>væn-djôh</rt><rb>死罪</rb><rt>si-ze</rt></ruby>。」<sup>67</sup> <ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>Keh-tsao</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>饞唾</rb><rt>zæn-tʽu</rt><rb>吐</rb><rt>tʽu</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>面孔</rb><rt>min-kʽong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>拳頭</rb><rt>gyün-deo</rt><rb>敲</rb><rt>kʽao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有星</rb><rt>yiu-sing</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>摑</rb><rt>kwah</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：<sup>68</sup> 「<u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>且</rb><rt>tsʽia</rt><rb>講講</rb><rt>kông-kông</rt><rb>先知</rb><rt>sin-cü</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>打</rb><rt>tang</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>誰</rb><rt>jü</rt></ruby>？」

<sup>\[1\]*ʽÔh-tsia* *wô*, pin-ts tang-Gyi. 或者話，鞭子打其。（原註）</sup>

<sup>69</sup> <u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>外頭</rb><rt>nga-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>明堂裏</rb><rt>ming-dông-li</rt><rb>坐間</rb><rt>zo-kæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>丫頭</rb><rt>ô-deo</rt><rb>走來</rb><rt>tseo-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>加利利</rb><rt>Kyüô-li-li</rt></ruby></u> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>同隊個</rb><rt>dong-de-go</rt></ruby>。」<sup>70</sup> <u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>衆人</rb><rt>cong-nying</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>招認</rb><rt>tsiao-jing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾個</rb><rt>Ng-go</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>咋個</rb><rt>dza-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt></ruby>。」<sup>71</sup> <u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>門口</rb><rt>meng-kʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>丫頭</rb><rt>*ô-deo*</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>來間個</rb><rt>læ-kæn-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>拿撒勒</rb><rt>Nô-sah-leh</rt></ruby></u> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>同隊個</rb><rt>dong-de-go</rt></ruby>。」<sup>72</sup> <u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>*Pe-teh*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>罰</rb><rt>vah</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>咒</rb><rt>tsiu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>第二遭</rb><rt>di-nyi-tsao</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>招認</rb><rt>tsiao-jing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>認得</rb><rt>nying-teh</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。」<sup>73</sup> <ruby><rb>過了</rb><rt>Ko-leh</rt><rb>一晌</rb><rt>ih-zông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>立東個</rb><rt>lih-tong-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>走來</rb><rt>tseo-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>實在</rb><rt>jih-dzæ</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>做隊</rb><rt>tso-de</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>口音</rb><rt>kʽeo-ing</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>辨勒</rb><rt>bin-leh</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>出</rb><rt>cʽih</rt></ruby>。」<sup>74</sup> <ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>Keh-tsao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>*Pe-teh*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>罰</rb><rt>vah</rt><rb>甚大咒</rb><rt>zing-do-tsiu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>認得</rb><rt>nying-teh</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>雞</rb><rt>Kyi</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>立刻</rb><rt>lih-kʽeh</rt><rb>啼了</rb><rt>di-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>75</sup> <u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>記得到</rb><rt>kyi-teh-tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>講過個</rb><rt>kông-ko-go</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>雞</rb><rt>Kyi</rt><rb>弗曾</rb><rt>feh-zing</rt><rb>啼個</rb><rt>di-go</rt><rb>前頭</rb><rt>zin-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>三遭</rb><rt>sæn-tsao</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>招認</rb><rt>tsiao-jing</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt><rb>外頭</rb><rt>nga-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>哭勒</rb><rt>kʽoh-leh</rt><rb>傷心</rb><rt>sông-sing</rt></ruby>。

---

### Mt. XXVII.

<ruby><rb>到了</rb><rt>TAO-LEH</rt><rb>天亮</rb><rt>tʽin-nyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>衆</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>祭司頭</rb><rt>tsi-s-deo</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>百姓個</rb><rt>pah-sing-go</rt><rb>長老</rb><rt>tsiang-lao</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>商量</rb><rt>siang-liang</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>辦</rb><rt>bæn</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>死罪</rb><rt>si-ze</rt></ruby>。<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>Ziu</rt><rb>縛仔</rb><rt>bo-ts</rt><rb>帶出去</rb><rt>ta-cʽih-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>解</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>總督</rb><rt>tsong-toh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>本丟</rb><rt>Peng-tiu</rt></ruby></u><u>·</u><u><ruby><rb>彼拉多</rb><rt>Pe-lah-to</rt></ruby></u>。

<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>賣</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>猶大</rb><rt>Yiu-da</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>罪</rb><rt>ze</rt><rb>定實</rb><rt>ding-jih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>懊悔</rb><rt>ao-hwe</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>馱勒</rb><rt>do-leh</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>三十塊</rb><rt>sæn-jih-kwʽe</rt><rb>銀子</rb><rt>nying-ts</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>還</rb><rt>wæn</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>祭司頭</rb><rt>tsi-s-deo</rt><rb>長老</rb><rt>tsiang-lao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：<sup>4</sup> 「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>賣</rb><rt>ma</rt><rb>嘸罪</rb><rt>m-ze</rt><rb>主顧個</rb><rt>Cü-kwu-go</rt><rb>血</rb><rt>hyüih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>罪過</rb><rt>ze-ko</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>與</rb><rt>Yü</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>啥個</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>相干</rb><rt>siang-ken</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>爾自</rb><rt>ng-zi</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>承當</rb><rt>dzing-tông</rt></ruby>。」<sup>5</sup> <u><ruby><rb>猶大</rb><rt>*Yiu-da*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>銀子</rb><rt>nying-ts</rt><rb>揞</rb><rt>kʽang</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>聖殿裏</rb><rt>*sing*-din-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>吊煞了</rb><rt>tiao-sah-de</rt></ruby>。

<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>祭司頭</rb><rt>tsi-s-deo</rt><rb>馱勒</rb><rt>do-leh</rt><rb>銀子</rb><rt>nying-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>既然</rb><rt>kyi-jün</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>賣血個</rb><rt>ma-hyüih-go</rt><rb>價鈿</rb><rt>kô-din</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>應該</rb><rt>ing-kæ</rt><rb>丟</rb><rt>tiu</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>銀庫裏</rb><rt>nying-kwʽu-li</rt></ruby>。」<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>Ziu</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>商量</rb><rt>siang-liang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>銀子</rb><rt>nying-ts</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>買</rb><rt>ma</rt><rb>燒窯個</rb><rt>siao-yiao-go</rt><rb>田</rb><rt>din</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>安葬</rb><rt>en-tsông</rt><rb>出門個</rb><rt>cʽih-meng-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>所以</rb><rt>Sô-yi</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>田</rb><rt>din</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>如今</rb><rt>jü-kying</rt><rb>謳</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby>「<ruby><rb>血田</rb><rt>Hyüih-din</rt></ruby>」。<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>Keh-tsao</rt><rb>先知</rb><rt>sin-cü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶利米</rb><rt>Yiæ-li-mi</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>講個</rb><rt>kông-go</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>效驗</rb><rt>yiao-nyiæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>馱勒</rb><rt>do-leh</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>三十塊</rb><rt>sæn-jih-kʽwe</rt><rb>銀子</rb><rt>nying-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>市值估價</rb><rt>z-dzih-kwu-kô</rt><rb>主顧個</rb><rt>cü-kwu-go</rt><rb>價鈿</rb><rt>kô-din</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>公估個</rb><rt>kong-kwu-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>馱出去</rb><rt>do-cʽih-kyʽi</rt><rb>買</rb><rt>ma</rt><rb>燒窯個</rb><rt>siao-yiao-go</rt><rb>田</rb><rt>din</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby>。」 

<sup>11</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>YIÆ-SU</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>立</rb><rt>lih</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>總督</rb><rt>tsong-toh</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>總督</rb><rt>tsong-toh</rt><rb>問</rb><rt>meng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>猶太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人個</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>皇帝</rb><rt>Wông-ti</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt></ruby>？」<u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>話着了</rb><rt>wô-djôh-de</rt></ruby>。」<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>祭司頭</rb><rt>tsi-s-deo</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>長老</rb><rt>tsiang-lao</rt><rb>告</rb><rt>kao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>一眼</rb><rt>ih-ngæn</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>應</rb><rt>ing</rt></ruby>。<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>Keh-tsao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>彼拉多</rb><rt>Pe-lah-to</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>多少</rb><rt>to-siao</rt><rb>案件</rb><rt>en-gyin</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>對證</rb><rt>te-tsing</rt><rb>告</rb><rt>kao</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>啥</rb><rt>soh</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>聽聞</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>嗎</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？」<sup>14</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>一樣</rb><rt>ih-yiang</rt><rb>案件</rb><rt>en-gyin</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>總督</rb><rt>tsong-toh</rt><rb>大樣</rb><rt>do-nyiang</rt><rb>稀奇</rb><rt>hyi-gyi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>Tao</rt><rb>節肯個</rb><rt>tsih-kʽeng-go</rt><rb>日子</rb><rt>*nyih-ts*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>總督</rb><rt>tsong-toh</rt><rb>老</rb><rt>lao</rt><rb>規矩</rb><rt>kwe-kyü</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>放</rb><rt>fông</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>犯人</rb><rt>væn-nying</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>隨</rb><rt>ze</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>如</rb><rt>jü</rt></ruby>。<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>有名個</rb><rt>yiu-ming-go</rt><rb>犯人</rb><rt>væn-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>巴拉巴</rb><rt>Pô-lah-pô</rt></ruby></u>。<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>百姓</rb><rt>Pah-sing</rt><rb>聚攏東</rb><rt>jü-long-tong</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>彼拉多</rb><rt>Pe-lah-to</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>放</rb><rt>fông</rt><rb>何裏</rb><rt>ʽah-li</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>還是</rb><rt>wa-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>巴拉巴</rb><rt>Pô-lah-pô</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>還是</rb><rt>wa-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u>？」<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>氣</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>弗過</rb><rt>feh-ko</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>解</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>上來個</rb><rt>zông-læ-go</rt></ruby>。

<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>總督</rb><rt>Tsong-toh</rt><rb>坐</rb><rt>zo</rt><rb>堂</rb><rt>dông</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>老𡢿</rb><rt>lao-nyüing</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>烏碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>正直</rb><rt>tsing-dzih</rt><rb>人個</rb><rt>Nying-go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>嘸得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>管賬</rb><rt>kwun-tsiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>今末</rb><rt>kyih-mih</rt><rb>夢</rb><rt>mong</rt><rb>裏向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>喫</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt><rb>許多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>苦頭</rb><rt>kwʽu-deo</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>祭司頭</rb><rt>tsi-s-deo</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>長老</rb><rt>tsiang-lao</rt><rb>攛掇</rb><rt>tsʽön-teh</rt><rb>箇潮</rb><rt>keh-dziao</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>討</rb><rt>tʽao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>巴拉巴</rb><rt>Pô-lah-pô</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>除滅</rb><rt>djü-mih</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u>。<sup>21</sup> <u><ruby><rb>彼拉多</rb><rt>Pe-lah-to</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>Ka-ni</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>該</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>辦</rb><rt>bæn</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>？」<ruby><rb>衆人</rb><rt>Cong-nying</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>Hao</rt><rb>釘</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>十字架上</rb><rt>jih-z-kô-zông</rt></ruby>。」<sup>23</sup> <ruby><rb>總督</rb><rt>Tsong-toh</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>啥個</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>惡</rb><rt>ôh</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>？」<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>越發</rb><rt>yüih-fah</rt><rb>謳勒</rb><rt>eo-leh</rt><rb>響</rb><rt>hyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>釘</rb><rt>Ting</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>十字架上</rb><rt>jih-z-kô-zông</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>24</sup> <u><ruby><rb>彼拉多</rb><rt>Pe-lah-to</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>嘸設法</rb><rt>m-shih-fah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>百姓</rb><rt>*pah-sing*</rt><rb>反響</rb><rt>fæn-hyiang</rt><rb>愈加</rb><rt>yü-kô</rt><rb>鬧事</rb><rt>nao-z</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>馱勒</rb><rt>do-leh</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>箇潮</rb><rt>keh-dziao</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>滰滰</rb><rt>gyiang-gyiang</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>流出</rb><rt>*Liu-cʽih*</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>正直</rb><rt>tsing-dzih</rt><rb>人個</rb><rt>Nying-go</rt><rb>血</rb><rt>hyüih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>嘸罪</rb><rt>m-ze</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>承當</rb><rt>dzing-tông</rt></ruby>。」<sup>25</sup> <ruby><rb>衆</rb><rt>Cong</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>其個</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>血</rb><rt>hyüih</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>歸</rb><rt>kwe</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>子孫</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt></ruby>。」<sup>26</sup> <ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>Keh-tsao</rt><rb>放</rb><rt>fông</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>巴拉巴</rb><rt>Pô-lah-pô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>鞭子</rb><rt>pin-ts</rt><rb>打</rb><rt>tang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>交代</rb><rt>kao-dæ</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>釘</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>十字架上</rb><rt>jih-z-kô-zông</rt></ruby>。

<sup>33</sup> <ruby><rb>走</rb><rt>TSEO</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>一𡍲</rb><rt>ih-tʽah</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>各各他</rb><rt>Kôh-kôh-tʽa</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby>『<ruby><rb>骷顱頭</rb><rt>kwʽu-lu-deo</rt><rb>烏碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt></ruby>』<ruby><rb>解說</rb><rt>ka-shih</rt></ruby>，<sup>34</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>馱勒</rb><rt>do-leh</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>苦膽</rb><rt>kwʽu-tæn</rt><rb>和的個</rb><rt>ʽo-tih-go</rt><rb>酸酒</rb><rt>sön-tsiu</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>喫</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>一</rb><rt>ih</rt><rb>嚐着</rb><rt>zông-djôh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>肯</rb><rt>kʽeng</rt><rb>喫</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt></ruby>。<sup>35</sup> <ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>先知人</rb><rt>sin-cü-nying</rt><rb>講過了</rb><rt>kông-ko-liao</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>效驗</rb><rt>yiao-nyiæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>外頭</rb><rt>nga-deo</rt><rb>衣裳</rb><rt>i-zông</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>分開</rb><rt>feng-kʽæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>裏頭</rb><rt>li-deo</rt><rb>衣裳</rb><rt>i-zông</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>撮</rb><rt>tsʽeh</rt><rb>指末頭</rb><rt>ts-meh-den</rt></ruby>。」
